------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE ajustalabels
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE ajustalabels
ThisForm.CfgTitOrigem = fRetLabels([Origem])
ThisForm.Label3.Caption = ThisForm.CfgTitOrigem
ThisForm.Grade.Grupo.Header1.Caption = ThisForm.CfgTitOrigem

ThisForm.CfgTitDestino = fRetLabels([Destino])
ThisForm.Label4.Caption = ThisForm.CfgTitDestino
ThisForm.Grade.Conta.Header1.Caption = ThisForm.CfgTitDestino

ThisForm.CfgTitVendedor	= fRetLabels([Vendedor])
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE buscapendencias
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE buscapendencias
Select * from TmpPends into cursor TmpPends2 order by datas, emps, dopes, numes readwrite
Zap in TmpPends

lcquery = [select cpros, qtbaixas, qtprods, units, qtds, empdopnums, cunis, codbarras, codbarras as codbarra2s from SigMvItn where 0=1 ]
If (ThisForm.parentform.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'LocalItens') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalItens)')
	Return 0
EndIf
Select TmpPends2
Scan
	lcepn = TmpPends2.Emps + TmpPends2.Dopes + Str(TmpPends2.Numes, 6)
	lcquery = [select a.cpros, a.qtbaixas, a.qtprods, a.units, a.qtds, a.empdopnums, a.codbarras, b.codbarras as codbarra2s from SigMvItn a ] +;
				[LEFT Join SigMvIts b On a.EmpDopNums = b.EmpDopNums And a.Citens = b.Citens where a.EmpDopNums = ?lcepn]
	If (ThisForm.parentform.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'LocalItens2') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalItens2)')
		Return 0
	EndIf
	Select LocalItens2
	Scan
		Scatter memo MEMVAR
		m.codbarra2s = NVL(m.codbarra2s,0) 
		Insert into LocalItens from memvar
	EndScan
EndScan

_CarObs       = Not( CrSigCdOpe.ConsClis = 2 )
_ChkQtdss = ( crSigCdOpe.ChkQtdss )
_BxParc   = ( crSigCdOpe.BxParcials )
lcobs = TprMvCab.obses

Select * from crTpmMvItn into cursor Baixa_eesti
Select Baixa_eesti
Scan
	If (Baixa_eesti.opers <> crSigOpCdd.opepadcs And Not Empty(crSigOpCdd.opepadcs)) Or Empty(Baixa_eesti.cpros)
		Loop
	EndIf

	_QtBaixar = Baixa_eesti.qtds

	Select TmpPends2
	Scan
		Scatter memo memvar

		lcepn = TmpPends2.Emps + TmpPends2.Dopes + Str(TmpPends2.Numes, 6)
				
		lcQuery = [Select Series,VlFretes,FrtIpis,FrtIcms,VlSeguros,SegIpis,SegIcms,DespAces,DesIpis,DesIcms,Cfis,nFis,EspecieNfs,emis From SigMvNfi Where EmpDopNums = ']+ lcepn +[']
		If Thisform.ParentForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpNfis') < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpNfis)')
			Select TmpOperacao
			Return .F.
		Endif

		lcSql = [Select a.nDopes From SigCdOpe a Where a.Dopes = ']+TmpPends2.Dopes+[']
		If Thisform.ParentForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'TmpOpeBus') < 1
			=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (TmpOpeBus)')
			Return .F.
		EndIf		

		Select LocalItens
		Locate for empdopnums = lcepn And Iif(crSigCdOpe.SemEtiqs<>2,cpros = Baixa_eesti.cpros,(codbarras = Baixa_eesti.codbarras Or codbarra2s = Baixa_eesti.codbarras))
		If !Eof('LocalItens') and _QtBaixar > 0 and (LocalItens.Qtbaixas <> LocalItens.Qtds)
			If !Seek(lcepn,'TmpPends','EmpDopNum')
				m.flags = .t.
				Insert into TmpPends from memvar
			EndIf
			
*!*				If LocalItens.QtProds + _QtBaixar <= LocalItens.Qtds
*!*					Replace QtProds With QtProds + _QtBaixar in LocalItens
*!*					_QtBaixado = _QtBaixar
*!*					_QtBaixar = 0
*!*				Else
*!*					_QtBaixar = _QtBaixar - ( LocalItens.Qtds - LocalItens.Qtbaixas )
*!*					_QtBaixado = LocalItens.Qtds - LocalItens.Qtbaixas
*!*					Replace QtProds With Qtds in LocalItens
*!*				EndIf
*!*				Replace qtbaixas with _QtBaixado in LocalItens

			If _BxParc = 3
				If LocalItens.QtProds + _QtBaixar <= LocalItens.Qtds
					Replace QtProds With QtProds + _QtBaixar in LocalItens
					_QtBaixado = _QtBaixar
					_QtBaixar = 0
				Else
					_QtBaixar = _QtBaixar - ( LocalItens.Qtds - LocalItens.Qtbaixas )
					_QtBaixado = LocalItens.Qtds - LocalItens.Qtbaixas
					Replace QtProds With Qtds in LocalItens
				EndIf
				Replace qtbaixas with _QtBaixado in LocalItens
			Else		
				If _ChkQtdss = 3 And ((Baixa_eesti.CodBarras > 0 And Baixa_eesti.CodBarras # LocalItens.CodBarras) Or (Baixa_eesti.CodBarras > 0 And Baixa_eesti.CodBarras # LocalItens.CodBarra2s))
					Loop
				EndIf
				If _ChkQtdss = 3 And Baixa_eesti.CodBarras = 0 And (LocalItens.CodBarras > 0 Or LocalItens.CodBarra2s > 0)
					Loop
				EndIf
				If LocalItens.QtBaixas + _QtBaixar <= LocalItens.Qtds
					Replace QtBaixas With QtBaixas + _QtBaixar in LocalItens
					_QtBaixado = _QtBaixar
					_QtBaixar = 0
				Else
					_QtBaixar = _QtBaixar - ( LocalItens.Qtds - LocalItens.QtBaixas )
					_QtBaixado = LocalItens.Qtds - LocalItens.QtBaixas
					Replace qtbaixas with LocalItens.qtds in LocalItens
				EndIf
			EndIf			
						
			nreg = Recno('Baixa_eesti')
*!*				If Baixa_eesti.units <> LocalItens.units && Tiago - 26/03/2014 - Comentei pois tem que desmembrar os itens na grade conforme o subnível que ele veio, e não agrupar por valor, pois estava perdendo o vínculo de qual subnível ele veio
				Select Baixa_eesti
				Scatter memo memvar				
				m.units = LocalItens.units
				m.nCodigos = Val( Str( TmpOpeBus.Ndopes, 4 ) + Transf( TmpPends2.Numes, '@L 999999' ) )	 && Tiago - 26/03/2014 - Não tava gravando o código do subnível que o item veio
							
				Select crTpmMvItn
				Go top
				Locate for citens = Baixa_eesti.citens
				replace qtds with qtds - _QtBaixado in crTpmMvItn
				If crTpmMvItn.qtds = 0 &&_QtBaixar > 0
*!*						replace qtds with _QtBaixar in crTpmMvItn
*!*					Else
					Delete From crTpmMvItn where citens = Baixa_eesti.citens
				EndIf				
				
				m.qtds = _QtBaixado
				Select Max(citens) as citens from crTpmMvItn into cursor Maxcitem
				ThisForm.CItem = Nvl(Maxcitem.citens,0)
				Select crTpmMvItn
				Go top
				Locate for cpros = LocalItens.cpros And nCodigos = m.nCodigos And units = LocalItens.units
				If Eof()
					m.citens = ThisForm.CItem + 1
					ThisForm.CItem = ThisForm.CItem + 1
					Insert into crTpmMvItn from memvar
				Else
					replace qtds with qtds + m.qtds in crTpmMvItn 
				Endif

				*** Tiago - 28/11/2013 - Alterado para buscar o crTpmMvIts para atualizar
				_QtdBaixar2 = _QtBaixado
				Select crTpmMvIts
*!*					Go top
*!*					Locate for citens = Baixa_eesti.citens
				Scan for citens = Baixa_eesti.citens And _QtdBaixar2 > 0
					Scatter Memo Memvar
					
					If _QtdBaixar2 <= crTpmMvIts.Qtds
						Replace Qtds With Qtds - _QtdBaixar2 in crTpmMvIts
						_QtBaixado = _QtdBaixar2					
						_QtdBaixar2 = 0
					Else
						_QtdBaixar2 = _QtdBaixar2 - crTpmMvIts.Qtds
						_QtBaixado = crTpmMvIts.Qtds

*!*							Replace Citens with ThisForm.CItem in crTpmMvIts
						Delete From crTpmMvIts &&where citens = Baixa_eesti.citens
					Endif
					
					If _QtBaixado > 0
						m.Qtds = _QtBaixado
						m.Citens = ThisForm.CItem
						Insert into crTpmMvIts from memvar						
					Endif
				Endscan
				
				Delete All For Qtds = 0 in crTpmMvIts
*!*				Else

*!*				EndIf

			If thisform.busprod and _CarObs
				Select crTpmMvItn
				Go top
				Locate for cpros = LocalItens.cpros and units = LocalItens.units
				If !Eof()
					thisform.parentform.calc_valite()
					lcobs = lcobs + 'DEV.P.SUA NF.' + crTmpNfis.nfis + ' ' + Dtoc(crTmpNfis.Emis) +  ' R$ ' + Alltrim(Transform(_QtBaixado * LocalItens.units,'@Z 9,999,999.99')) + ;
							' SENDO REL. ' + Alltrim(Transform(_QtBaixado,'@Z 999,999,999.999')) + ' ' + crTpmMvItn.cunis + ' ' + Alltrim(crTpmMvItn.dpros) + ' - '

				EndIf
			EndIf			
		EndIf
	EndScan
EndScan

If thisform.busprod
	replace Obses with lcobs in TprMvCab
EndIf
		
lnCount = 0
Set Order To
Select crTpmMvItn
Scan
	lnCount = lnCount + 1
	Select crTpmMvIts
	Set Order To
	Set Order to CItens
	Seek crTpmMvItn.CItens
	Scan While CItens = crTpmMvItn.CItens
		Replace CItens With lnCount
	EndScan

	Select crSigSubMv
	Set Order To
	Set Order to CItem2
	Seek crTpmMvItn.CItens
	Scan While CItem2 = crTpmMvItn.CItens
		Replace CItem2 With lnCount
	EndScan

	Select crTpmMvItn
	Replace crTpmMvItn.CItens With lnCount
EndScan	
ThisForm.CItem = lnCount

Select crTpmMvItn
Scan
	If !Empty(crTpmMvItn.cpros)
		thisform.parentform.calc_valite()
	EndIf
EndScan 
Select TmpPends
Set Order To tag datas
Scan
	thisform.marca()
EndScan

Return .t.
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE carregar
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE carregar
Lparameters pCodigo, pNota, pPedido, pIdTranp, pCpros, pFiltraTempo

Local lnChkCta

Thisform.LiberaTimer = .F.

xDAteT = Datetime()
Do While Datetime() - xDAteT < 2

Enddo

llFiltraGrupo = .F.
*** mena - 25/03/2017 - seleciona grupos de produto acababado configurado na operação ***
lcSql = [select a.cpros from sigcdpro a, sigcdgps b where b.dopes = ']+TprMvCab.dopes+[' and a.cgrus = b.cgrus]
If Thisform.parentform.podataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'TmpcdGps') < 1
	=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão carregar (TmpcdGps)')
	Return 0
Endif
If Reccount('TmpcdGps')>0
	llFiltraGrupo = .T.
Endif

lcSql = [Select * From SigCdOpe Where dopes = ']+TprMvCab.dopes+[']
If Thisform.parentform.podataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrSigCdOpe') < 1
	=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão carregar (crSigCdOpe)')
	Return 0
EndIf

lcSql = [Select * From SigOpCdc Where dopes = ']+TprMvCab.dopes+[']
If Thisform.parentform.podataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrSigOpCdc') < 1
	=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão carregar (CrSigOpCdc)')
	Return 0
Endif

lcSql = [Select * From SigOpCdd Where dopes = ']+TprMvCab.dopes+[']
If Thisform.parentform.podataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrSigOpCdd') < 1
	=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão carregar (CrSigOpCdd)')
	Return 0
Endif

lcSql = [Select * From SigOpCdI Where dopes = ']+TprMvCab.dopes+[']
If Thisform.parentform.podataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrSigOpCdI') < 1
	=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão carregar (CrSigOpCdI)')
	Return 0
Endif

lcSql = [Select * From SigCdEmp Where Cemps = ']+TprMvCab.Emps+[']
If (Thisform.parentform.podataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql, [CrSigCdEmp]) < 1)
	=Messagebox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão carregar (CrSigCdEmp)])
	Return .F.
EndIf

_DopesNivel = crSigCdOpe.Nivels
_Opera = crSigCdOpe.Opers
_PuxaOrigem = crSigCdOpe.ConOSubs

*-- Rafael - 12/04/2017 - Valida a quantidade dos pedidos com um determinado status
_QtdsStatus = [] &&Iif(crSigOpCdd.StaRepros = 1,['R'],[])
If !Empty(crSigOpCdi.qtdssts)
	For x=1 To Len(Alltrim(crSigOpCdi.qtdssts))
		_QtdsStatus = _QtdsStatus+ Iif(Empty(_QtdsStatus),[']+Substr(Alltrim(crSigOpCdi.qtdssts),x,1)+['],[,']+Substr(Alltrim(crSigOpCdi.qtdssts),x,1)+['])
	Next
Endif

_EmpDesMov  = crSigOpCdd.EmpDesMov

*** mena 27/10/2015 incluido 5 e 6
*!*_PuxaDestino = IIF(INLIST(crSigCdOpe.CupFis,1,5,6), 3, crSigCdOpe.ConDSubs)	&& Mena 21/03/2014 para carregar subnivel independente de conta de destino
lnChkCta = Int(Val(Substr(crSigOpCdd.ConfigGers, 238,1)))	&& mena 19/10/2016 - checa cliente exatamente ou não
_PuxaDestino = Iif(Inlist(crSigCdOpe.CupFis,1,5,6) And (Empty(TprMvCab.contads) Or lnChkCta=0), 3, crSigCdOpe.ConDSubs)	&& Mena 21/03/2014 para carregar subnivel independente de conta de destino
_SubnB = crSigCdOpe.SubNs=3 And CrSigOpCdc.NCarBxs = 1

* Richard em 01/07/2015 - Carregar Somente Subníveis Cancelados
llCan = (Int(Val(Substr(crSigOpCdd.ConfigGers, 216, 1))) = 1)

llSubnt  = .F.
llPagoT  = .T.
llPagoF  = .F.
llSubntT = .T.
lnDiaPd  = Iif(Thisform.Validardias,Thisform.Get_meses.Value,0)
LnMesPd  = Iif(Thisform.Validardias,0,Thisform.Get_meses.Value)
llDtDD   = fDtoSQL(Date()+1-lnDiaPd)
llDtMM   = fDtoSQL(Gomonth(Date(),LnMesPd*-1))
lcWhr    = [ ]
llValFpg = .F.

If (Thisform.parentform.podataMgr.SqlExecute([Select Itras From SigOpTrp Where Dopes = ']+TprMvCab.Dopes+['], [LocalTrp]) < 1)
	=Messagebox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (LocalTrp)])
	Return .F.
EndIf

llTrp = []
Select LocalTrp
Scan
	llTrp = llTrp + Iif(!Empty(llTrp),[,'],[']) + LocalTrp.Itras + [']
	Select LocalTrp
Endscan
Use In LocalTrp

IF Type([crSigOpCdi.VldEmpMov])=[N] And crSigOpCdi.VldEmpMov = 1
	llCemp = [('] + TprMvCab.Emps + [')]
Else
	*** Tiago - 03/11/2014 - CC2016 - Checo as empresas que o usuário tem acesso, e só traz as movimentações dessas empresas
	If (Thisform.parentform.podataMgr.SqlExecute([Select Cemps From SigCdEmp Where Ativas = 1], [LocalEmp]) < 1)
		=Messagebox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (LocalEmp)])
		Return .F.
	Endif
	llCemp = []

	Select LocalEmp
	Scan
		llOk = fAcessoEmpresa(Usuar,'C',LocalEmp.Cemps,'','',Thisform.podataMgr,.T.) Or Inlist(crSigOpCdd.EmpSubnAs,0,2)	&& mena 17/11/2014 - incluido flag para não checar acesso de empresa
		If llOk
			llCemp = llCemp + Iif(!Empty(llCemp),[,'],[']) + LocalEmp.Cemps + [']
		Endif
	Endscan
	llCemp = Iif(!Empty(llCemp),[(] + llCemp + [)],[])
	Use In LocalEmp
EndIf 

lcFiltroTempo = []
lcFiltroTempo2 = []
If not pFiltraTempo
	If Type([CrSigOpCdI.ValTmpSubn])=[N] And CrSigOpCdI.ValTmpSubn = 1
		If Type([CrSigCdEmp.TempoSubn])=[N] And CrSigCdEmp.TempoSubn > 0
			lcFiltroTempo = [ And datediff(minute,dtalts,getdate()) <= ] + Alltrim(Str(CrSigCdEmp.TempoSubn)) + [ ] 
			lcFiltroTempo2 = [ And datediff(minute,a.dtalts,getdate()) <= ] + Alltrim(Str(CrSigCdEmp.TempoSubn)) + [ ] 
		EndIf 
	EndIf 
EndIf 
***

lcFilVal = []
lcFilVal2 = []
If Type([CrSigOpCdI.MovComVal])=[N] And CrSigOpCdI.MovComVal = 1
	lcFilVal = [ And Valos <> 0 ] 
	lcFilVal2= [ And a.Valos <> 0 ]
EndIf 

Select TmpPends
Set Order To EmpDopNum

If (crSigCdOpe.SubNs=3 And Not Empty(_DopesNivel)) Or crSigCdOpe.Subns = 1

	Thisform.Caption = 'Operações Pendentes ( '+Alltrim(_DopesNivel)+' )'

	*** Tiago - 08/06/2011 - Checa status permitos na operação
	lcStatus = [] &&Iif(crSigOpCdd.StaRepros = 1,['R'],[])
	If !Empty(crSigOpCdd.StsSubs)
		For x=1 To Len(Alltrim(crSigOpCdd.StsSubs))
			lcStatus = lcStatus + Iif(Empty(lcStatus),[']+Substr(Alltrim(crSigOpCdd.StsSubs),x,1)+['],[,']+Substr(Alltrim(crSigOpCdd.StsSubs),x,1)+['])
		Next
	Endif

	* Checagem de Teste de Richard Para Carat Em 17/10/2011 // A Checagem Vai até o ELSE desse Inlist
	* A intenção é que realize o Filtro dos Fornecedores No Momento da Seleção Agilizando o Processo.
	If Inlist(crSigOpCdd.ChkIfors, 1, 2)
		lcFor = Iif(crSigOpCdd.ChkIfors = 1, TprMvCab.ContaOs, TprMvCab.contads)

		lcQuery = [Select a.Emps, a.dopes, a.Numes, a.GrupoOs, a.ContaOs, a.GrupoDs, a.ContaDs, a.lCancelas, a.ChkSubn, a.ChkBxParcs, a.ObsCabMovs, Jobs,]+;
			[a.MascNum, a.Datas, a.Valos, a.Notas, a.Obses, a.TpFats, a.Usuars, a.EmpDs, a.pStatus, a.PrazoEnts, a.Vends, a.ChaveNota, a.IDTransp, a.NumeroNota, a.EmpRet ] + ;
			[From SigMvCab a, SigMvItn b, SigCdPro c ]

		If crSigCdOpe.EmpSubns = 1
			lcQuery = lcQuery + [Where a.Dopes = '] + _DopesNivel + [' And a.Emps in ] + llCemp + [ And ]
		Else
			lcEmp = Padr(Iif(Not Empty(crSigCdOpe.EmpBxs), crSigCdOpe.EmpBxs, TprMvCab.Emps), 3)
			lcQuery = lcQuery + [Where a.EmpDopNums BetWeen "] + ;
				lcEmp + Padr(_DopesNivel,26) + [" And "] + lcEmp + Padr(_DopesNivel,20) + [999999" And a.Emps in ] + llCemp + [ And ]
		Endif

		* Richard em 01/07/2015 - Carregar Somente Subníveis Cancelados, incluindo a linha: Iif(llCan,[a.LCancelas = ?llSubntT And ],[])
		lcQuery = lcQuery + ;
			Iif(lnDiaPd>0,[a.Datas >= ?llDtDD And ],[ ] )+;
			Iif(LnMesPd>0,[a.Datas >= ?llDtMM And ],[ ])+;
			Iif(crSigCdOpe.Subns = 1,[a.ChkSubn = ?llsubnt And ],Iif(_SubnB,[a.ChkSubn = ?llSubntT And ],[ ]))+;
			Iif(llCan,[a.LCancelas = ?llSubntT And ],[])+;
			Iif(crSigCdOpe.ChecPagos = 1,[a.ChkPagos = ?llPagoT And ],Iif(crSigCdOpe.ChecPagos = 2,[a.ChkPagos = ?llPagoF And ],[ ]))+;
			Iif(crSigCdOpe.ChkAgends = 1,[Not a.DtAgends is Null And ],Iif(crSigCdOpe.ChkAgends = 3,[a.DtAgends is Null And ],[ ]))+;
			Iif(CrSigOpCdc.MercSeps = 1,[a.Utilizados = 1 And ], Iif(CrSigOpCdc.MercSeps = 3,[a.utilizados = 0 And ], [ ] ) ) +;
			Iif(!Empty(lcStatus),[a.pStatus In (]+lcStatus+[) And ],[]) +;
			Iif(Inlist(Int(Val(Substr(crSigOpCdi.lbltxidCs, 25, 1))),1,3) And !Empty(TprMvCab.Jobs),[a.Jobs = '] + TprMvCab.Jobs + [' And ],[]) +;
			[a.EmpDopNums = b.EmpDopNums And b.CPros = c.CPros And ] +;
			[c.IFors = '] + lcFor + ['] + ;
			lcFiltroTempo2 + lcFilVal2
	Else
		If crSigCdOpe.EmpSubns = 1
			* Richard em 01/07/2015 - Carregar Somente Subníveis Cancelados, incluindo a linha: Iif(llCan,[a.LCancelas = ?llSubntT And ],[])
			lcQuery = [Select Emps, dopes, Numes, GrupoOs, ContaOs, GrupoDs, ContaDs, lCancelas, ChkSubn, ChkBxParcs, ObsCabMovs, Jobs, ]+;
				[MascNum, Datas, Valos, Notas, Obses, TpFats, Usuars, EmpDs, pStatus, PrazoEnts, Vends, PedidoWeb, IDEntrega, ChaveNota, ] + ;
				[IDTransp, NumeroNota, EmpRet ] + ;
				[From SigMvCab Where Dopes = ']+_DopesNivel+[' ]+;
				[And Emps in ] + llCemp + [ ] +;
				Iif(lnDiaPd>0,[ And Datas >= ?llDtDD ],[ ] )+;
				Iif(LnMesPd>0,[ And Datas >= ?llDtMM ],[ ])+;
				Iif(crSigCdOpe.Subns = 1,[ And ChkSubn = ?llsubnt ],Iif(_SubnB,[ And ChkSubn = ?llSubntT ],[ ]))+;
				Iif(llCan,[ And LCancelas = ?llSubntT ],[])+;
				Iif(crSigCdOpe.ChecPagos = 1,[ And ChkPagos = ?llPagoT ],Iif(crSigCdOpe.ChecPagos = 2,[ And ChkPagos = ?llPagoF ],[ ]))+;
				Iif(crSigCdOpe.ChkAgends = 1,[ And Not DtAgends is Null ],Iif(crSigCdOpe.ChkAgends = 3,[ And DtAgends is Null ],[ ]))+;
				Iif(CrSigOpCdc.MercSeps = 1,[ And Utilizados = 1 ], Iif(CrSigOpCdc.MercSeps = 3,[ And utilizados = 0 ], [ ] ) ) +;
				Iif(Inlist(Int(Val(Substr(crSigOpCdi.lbltxidCs, 25, 1))),1,3) And !Empty(TprMvCab.Jobs),[ And Jobs = '] + TprMvCab.Jobs + ['],[]) +;
				Iif(!Empty(lcStatus),[ And pStatus in (]+lcStatus+[)],[]) + ;
				Iif(!Empty(llTrp),[ And IdTransp In (] + llTrp + [)],[])  + ;
				lcFiltroTempo + lcFilVal
		Else
			lcQuery = [Select Emps, dopes, Numes, GrupoOs, ContaOs, GrupoDs, ContaDs, lCancelas, ChkSubn, ChkBxParcs, ObsCabMovs, Jobs, ]+;
				[MascNum, Datas, Valos, Notas, Obses, TpFats, Usuars, EmpDs, pStatus, PrazoEnts, Vends, PedidoWeb, IDEntrega, ChaveNota, ] + ;
				[IDTransp, NumeroNota, EmpRet ] + ;
				[From SigMvCab ] + ;
				[Where empdopnums between "]+;
				Padr(Iif(!Empty(crSigCdOpe.EmpBxs),crSigCdOpe.EmpBxs,TprMvCab.Emps),3)+Padr(_DopesNivel,26)+[" and "]+;
				Padr(Iif(!Empty(crSigCdOpe.EmpBxs),crSigCdOpe.EmpBxs,TprMvCab.Emps),3)+Padr(_DopesNivel,20)+Str(999999,6)+[" ]+;
				[And Emps in ] + llCemp	+ [ ] +;
				Iif(lnDiaPd>0,[ And Datas >= ?llDtDD ],[ ] )+;
				Iif(LnMesPd>0,[ And Datas >= ?llDtMM ],[ ])+;
				Iif(crSigCdOpe.Subns = 1,[ And ChkSubn = ?llsubnt ],Iif(_SubnB,[And ChkSubn = ?llSubntT ],[ ]))+;
				Iif(llCan,[ And LCancelas = ?llSubntT ],[])+;
				Iif(crSigCdOpe.ChecPagos = 1,[ And ChkPagos = ?llPagoT ],Iif(crSigCdOpe.ChecPagos = 2,[ And ChkPagos = ?llPagoF ],[ ]))+;
				Iif(crSigCdOpe.ChkAgends = 1,[ And Not DtAgends is Null],Iif(crSigCdOpe.ChkAgends = 3,[ And DtAgends is Null ],[ ]))+;
				Iif(CrSigOpCdc.MercSeps = 1,[ And Utilizados = 1 ], Iif(CrSigOpCdc.MercSeps = 3,[ And utilizados = 0 ], [ ] ) ) +;
				Iif(Inlist(Int(Val(Substr(crSigOpCdi.lbltxidCs, 25, 1))),1,3) And !Empty(TprMvCab.Jobs),[ And Jobs = '] + TprMvCab.Jobs + ['],[]) +;
				Iif(!Empty(lcStatus),[ And pStatus in (]+lcStatus+[)],[]) + ;
				Iif(!Empty(llTrp),[ And IdTransp In (] + llTrp + [)],[]) + ;
				lcFiltroTempo + lcFilVal
		Endif
	Endif

	If (Thisform.parentform.podataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'creest') < 1)
		=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (creest)')
		Thisform.LiberaTimer = .T.
		Return 0
	Endif

	Create Cursor oPendSnope (cNivels c(20), TpFats c(3), tStatus N(1), CkAtCta N(1))

Else

	Thisform.Caption = 'Operações Pendentes ( * )'

	Select TprMvCab
	=Afields(v01)
	Create Cursor CrEest From Array v01
	
	lcQuery = [Select * From SigCdFpN Where Dopes = ']+TprMvCab.dopes+[' ]
	If (Thisform.parentform.podataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'oPendFpn') < 1)
		=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (oPendFpn)')
		Thisform.LiberaTimer = .T.
		Return 0
	Endif

	Select oPendFpn
	If Reccount()>0
		llValFpg = .T.
	EndIf 

	lcQuery = [Select cNivels, TpFats, tStatus, CkAtCta From SigOpCsn Where Cdopes = ']+TprMvCab.dopes+[' And cNivels <> ' ']
	If (Thisform.parentform.podataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'oPendSnope') < 1)
		=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (oPendSnope)')
		Thisform.LiberaTimer = .T.
		Return 0
	Endif

	Select oPendSnope
	lcDopNiv = ''
	Scan
		lcDopNiv = Iif(Empty(lcDopNiv),[],lcDopNiv+[,]) + [']+oPendSnope.cNivels+[']
	Endscan

	*** Tiago - 08/06/2011 - Checa status permitos na operação
	lcStatus = [] &&Iif(crSigOpCdd.StaRepros = 1,['R'],[])
	If !Empty(crSigOpCdd.StsSubs)
		For x=1 To Len(Alltrim(crSigOpCdd.StsSubs))
			lcStatus = lcStatus + Iif(Empty(lcStatus),[']+Substr(Alltrim(crSigOpCdd.StsSubs),x,1)+['],[,']+Substr(Alltrim(crSigOpCdd.StsSubs),x,1)+['])
		Next
	Endif

	*** Tiago - 10/06/2013 - Kleber Jóias - A intenção é que realize o Filtro dos Fornecedores No Momento da Seleção Agilizando o Processo.
	If Inlist(crSigOpCdd.ChkIfors, 1, 2)
		lcFor = Iif(crSigOpCdd.ChkIfors = 1, TprMvCab.ContaOs, TprMvCab.contads)

		* Richard em 01/07/2015 - Carregar Somente Subníveis Cancelados, incluindo a linha: Iif(llCan,[a.LCancelas = ?llSubntT And ],[])
		lcQuery = [Select a.Emps, a.dopes, a.Numes, a.GrupoOs, a.ContaOs, a.GrupoDs, a.ContaDs, a.lCancelas, a.ChkSubn, a.ChkBxParcs, a.ObsCabMovs, a.Jobs, ]+;
			[a.MascNum, a.Datas, a.Valos, a.Notas, a.Obses, a.TpFats, a.CodTrans, a.LocalEnts, a.Usuars, a.EmpDs, a.pStatus, a.PrazoEnts, a.Vends, ] + ;
			[a.PedidoWeb, a.IDEntrega, a.ChaveNota, a.IDTransp, a.NumeroNota, a.EmpRet ] +;
			[From SigMvCab a, SigMvItn b, SigCdPro c Where ]+;
			[a.Dopes in (]+lcDopNiv+[) ]+;
			[And a.Emps in ] + llCemp	 + [ ] +;
			Iif(lnDiaPd > 0 ,[ And a.Datas >= ?llDtDD ],[ ] )+;
			Iif(LnMesPd > 0 And pCodigo=0 And Empty(pNota),[ And a.Datas >= ?llDtMM ],[ ])+;
			Iif(crSigCdOpe.SubNs=3,;
			Iif(_SubnB And crSigOpCdI.ncarbxpar = 1,[ And (a.ChkSubn = ?llSubntT or a.ChkBxParcs = 1) ],;
			Iif(_SubnB,[ And a.ChkSubn = ?llSubntT ],[ ])),[And a.ChkSubn = ?llsubnt ])+;
			Iif(llCan,[ And a.LCancelas = ?llSubntT ],[])+;
			Iif(pCodigo>0,[ And a.Numes = ]+Str(pCodigo,6)+[ ],[])+;
			Iif(Not Empty(pNota),[ And a.Notas = ']+pNota+[' ],[])+;
			Iif(Not Empty(pPedido),[ And a.PedidoWeb = ']+pPedido+[' ],[])+;
			Iif(Not Empty(pIdTranp),[ And a.IdTransp = ']+pIdTranp+[' ],[])+;
			Iif(Not Empty(pCpros),[ And b.Cpros = ']+pCpros+[' ],[])+;
			Iif(crSigCdOpe.EmpSubns=1,[ ],[ And a.Emps = ']+Padr(Iif( Not Empty(crSigCdOpe.EmpBxs),crSigCdOpe.EmpBxs,TprMvCab.Emps),3)+['])+[ ]+;
			Iif(_EmpDesMov<>1,[ ],[ And a.EmpDs = ']+TprMvCab.Emps+['])+[ ]+;
			Iif(crSigCdOpe.ChecPagos = 1,[ And a.ChkPagos = ?llPagoT ],Iif(crSigCdOpe.ChecPagos = 2,[ And a.ChkPagos = ?llPagoF ],[ ]))+;
			Iif(crSigCdOpe.ChkAgends = 1,[ And Not a.DtAgends is Null ],Iif(crSigCdOpe.ChkAgends = 3,[ And a.DtAgends is Null ],[ ]))+;
			Iif(CrSigOpCdc.MercSeps = 1,[ And a.Utilizados = 1 ], Iif(CrSigOpCdc.MercSeps = 3,[ And a.utilizados = 0 ], [ ] ) ) +;
			Iif(_SubnB And crSigOpCdI.ncarbxpar = 1,[],Iif(crSigOpCdI.ncarbxpar = 1,[ And a.ChkBxParcs = 1 ], [ ])) +;
			Iif(Inlist(Int(Val(Substr(crSigOpCdd.lbltxidCs, 25, 1))),1,3) And !Empty(TprMvCab.Jobs),[ And a.Jobs = '] + TprMvCab.Jobs + ['],[]) +;
			Iif(!Empty(lcStatus),[ And a.pStatus in (]+lcStatus+[) ],[ ]) + ;
			Iif(!Empty(llTrp),[ And a.IdTransp In (] + llTrp + [)],[]) + ;
			Iif(llValFpg,[And a.empdopnums in(select EmpDopNums from SigMvPar a join SigCdFpN b on a.fpags = b.fpags where b.dopes = ']+TprMvCab.dopes+[')],[]) + ;
			[And a.EmpDopNums = b.EmpDopNums And ] + ;
			[b.CPros = c.CPros And ] + ;
			[c.IFors = '] + lcFor + ['] + ;
			lcFiltroTempo2 + lcFilVal2
	Else
		*-- 				  Iif(CrSigCdOpe.Subns = 3,Iif(_SubnB,[ And ChkSubn = ?llSubntT ],[ ]),[And ChkSubn = ?llsubnt ])+;

		lcQuery = [Select distinct a.Emps, a.dopes, a.Numes, a.GrupoOs, a.ContaOs, a.GrupoDs, a.ContaDs, a.lCancelas, a.ChkSubn, a.ChkBxParcs, a.ObsCabMovs, a.Jobs, ]+;
			[a.MascNum, a.Datas, a.Valos, a.Notas, convert(varchar(max),a.Obses) as Obses, a.TpFats, a.CodTrans, a.LocalEnts, a.Usuars, a.EmpDs, a.pStatus, a.PrazoEnts, ] + ;
			[a.Vends, a.PedidoWeb, a.IDEntrega, a.ChaveNota, a.IDTransp, a.NumeroNota, a.EmpRet ] + ;
			[From SigMvCab a ] + ;
			[left join SigMvItn b On a.empdopnums = b.empdopnums ] + ;
			[Where a.Dopes in (]+lcDopNiv+[) ]+;
			[And a.Emps in ] + llCemp	 + [ ] +;
			Iif(lnDiaPd > 0 ,[ And a.Datas >= ?llDtDD ],[ ] )+;
			Iif(LnMesPd > 0 And pCodigo=0 And Empty(pNota),[ And a.Datas >= ?llDtMM ],[ ])+;
			Iif(crSigCdOpe.SubNs=3,Iif(_SubnB And crSigOpCdI.ncarbxpar = 1,[ And (a.ChkSubn = ?llSubntT or a.ChkBxParcs = 1) ],Iif(_SubnB,[ And a.ChkSubn = ?llSubntT ],[ ])),[And a.ChkSubn = ?llsubnt ])+;
			Iif(llCan,[ And a.LCancelas = ?llSubntT ],[])+;
			Iif(pCodigo>0,[ And a.Numes = ]+Str(pCodigo,6)+[ ],[])+;
			Iif(Not Empty(pNota),[ And a.Notas = ']+pNota+[' ],[])+;
			Iif(Not Empty(pPedido),[ And a.PedidoWeb = ']+pPedido+[' ],[])+;
			Iif(Not Empty(pIdTranp),[ And a.IdTransp = ']+pIdTranp+[' ],[])+;
			Iif(Not Empty(pCpros),[ And b.Cpros = ']+pCpros+[' ],[])+;
			Iif(crSigCdOpe.EmpSubns=1,Iif(_EmpDesMov=1,[ And a.EmpDs = ']+TprMvCab.Emps+['],[ ]),;
					[ And (a.Emps = ']+Padr(Iif( Not Empty(crSigCdOpe.EmpBxs),crSigCdOpe.EmpBxs,TprMvCab.Emps),3)+[' ]+Iif(_EmpDesMov=1,[ Or a.EmpDs = ']+TprMvCab.Emps+['],[ ])+[ )])+[ ]+;
			Iif(crSigCdOpe.ChecPagos = 1,[ And a.ChkPagos = ?llPagoT ],Iif(crSigCdOpe.ChecPagos = 2,[ And a.ChkPagos = ?llPagoF ],[ ]))+;
			Iif(crSigCdOpe.ChkAgends = 1,[ And Not a.DtAgends is Null ],Iif(crSigCdOpe.ChkAgends = 3,[ And a.DtAgends is Null ],[ ]))+;
			Iif(CrSigOpCdc.MercSeps = 1,[ And a.Utilizados = 1 ], Iif(CrSigOpCdc.MercSeps = 3,[ And a.utilizados = 0 ], [ ] ) ) +;
			Iif(_SubnB And crSigOpCdI.ncarbxpar = 1,[],Iif(crSigOpCdI.ncarbxpar = 1,[ And a.ChkBxParcs = 1 ], [ ] )) +;
			Iif(Inlist(Int(Val(Substr(crSigOpCdi.lbltxidCs, 25, 1))),1,3) And !Empty(TprMvCab.Jobs),[ And a.Jobs = '] + TprMvCab.Jobs + ['],[]) +;
			Iif(!Empty(lcStatus),[ And a.pStatus in (]+lcStatus+[)],[]) + ;
			Iif(!Empty(llTrp),[ And a.IdTransp In (] + llTrp + [)],[]) + ;
			Iif(llValFpg,[And a.empdopnums in(select EmpDopNums from SigMvPar a join SigCdFpN b on a.fpags = b.fpags where b.dopes = ']+TprMvCab.dopes+[')],[]) + ;
			iif(llFiltraGrupo,[And b.cpros in(select a.cpros from sigcdpro a, sigcdgps b where b.dopes = ']+TprMvCab.dopes+[' and a.cgrus = b.cgrus) ],[]) + ;
			lcFiltroTempo2 + lcFilVal2
	Endif

	If (Thisform.parentform.podataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrEest') < 1)
		=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (xcreest)')
		Thisform.LiberaTimer = .T.
		Return 0
	Endif

Endif

Select oPendSnope
Index On cNivels Tag dopes

Thisform.Refresh
Create Cursor CrContas (Iclis c(10), Rclis c(40))
Index On Iclis Tag Iclis

lcDp = Space(20)
Select CrEest
_ct = Reccount()
_ca = 0
Scan
	_ca = _ca + 1
	If IsEmpty(CrEest.dopes) Or Seek(CrEest.Emps + CrEest.dopes + Str(CrEest.Numes,6),'TmpPends')
		Loop
	Endif

	If CrEest.dopes # lcDp
		lcDp = CrEest.dopes
		lcSql = [Select Dopes,nDopes From SigCdOpe Where dopes = ']+lcDp+[']
		If Thisform.parentform.podataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrTmpOpe') < 1
			=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão carregar (crSigCdOpe)')
			Thisform.LiberaTimer = .T.
			Return 0
		Endif
	Endif

	If CrEest.Empds # TprMvCab.Empds And crSigCdOpe.CempSubns = 1
		Loop
	Endif

	If CrEest.Empds # TprMvCab.Emps And crSigCdOpe.CempSubns = 7
		Loop
	Endif

	If ( crSigOpCdd.Contaods<>' ' And Inlist(crSigOpCdd.OptCts,1,2,3) )
		If Not (( CrEest.ContaOs = crSigOpCdd.Contaods And Inlist(crSigOpCdd.OptCts,1,3) ) Or ;
				( CrEest.contads = crSigOpCdd.Contaods And Inlist(crSigOpCdd.OptCts,2,3) ) )
			Loop
		Endif
	Endif

	=Seek(CrEest.dopes,'OpendSnOpe','Dopes')
	If Not Empty(oPendSnope.TpFats) And oPendSnope.TpFats <> CrEest.TpFats
		Loop
	Endif

	*** Tiago - 05/09/2014 - MULT - Filtrar subnível pelo adendo do tipo de faturamento
	If !Empty(Substr(crSigOpCdd.ConfigGers, 195, 02))
		lcSql = [Select Codigos From SigCdTif Where Adendocs = '] + Substr(crSigOpCdd.ConfigGers, 195, 02) + [' And Codigos = ']+CrEest.TpFats+[']
		If Thisform.parentform.podataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrSigCdTif') < 1
			=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão carregar (crSigCdTif)')
			Return .F.
		Endif
		If Eof()
			Loop
		Endif
	Endif
	
	*-- Rafael - 12/04/2017 - Valida se a quantidade da movimentação é igual as movimentações que estão com o mesmo numero de pedido/status do filtro
	If Not Empty(_QtdsStatus) And Not Empty(CrEest.PedidoWeb)
		lcEDN = CrEest.Emps + CrEest.Dopes + Str(CrEest.Numes,6)
		lcQuery = [Select isnull(Sum(Qtds),0) as Qtds, a.EmpDopNums ] + ;
				[from SigMvCab a ] + ;
				[Join SigMvItn b on a.EmpDopNums =  b.EmpDopNums ] + ;
				[Where a.EmpDopNums = ']+lcEDN+[' ] + ;
				[group by a.EmpDopNums ]
		If Thisform.parentform.podataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpMov') < 1
			=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão carregar (CrTmpMov)')
			Return .F.
		EndIf
		
		Select CrTmpMov
		lnQtds = Nvl(CrTmpMov.Qtds,0)
		
		lcQuery = [Select isnull(Sum(Qtds),0) as Qtds, b.PedidoWeb ] + ;
				[from SigMvCab a ] + ;
				[Join SigMvItn b on a.EmpDopNums =  b.EmpDopNums ] + ;
				[Where b.PedidoWeb = ']+CrEest.PedidoWeb+[' ] + ;
				[And a.pStatus in (]+_QtdsStatus+[) ] + ;
				[And a.EmpDopNums <> ']+lcEDN+[' ] + ;
				[group by b.PedidoWeb ]
		If Thisform.parentform.podataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpSts') < 1
			=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão carregar (CrTmpSts)')
			Return .F.
		EndIf
		
		Select CrTmpSts
		If Nvl(CrTmpSts.Qtds,0) <> lnQtds
			Select CrEest
			Loop 
		EndIf 		
	EndIf 
	
	Select CrEest
	_NumeroNivel = Str( crTmpOpe.NDopes, 4 )

	_Flag =  Seek( CrEest.Emps + Str(Val( _NumeroNivel + Transf( CrEest.Numes, '@L 999999' ) ),10), 'TmpOperacao', 'Codigos' )

	If Thisform.Op_Escolha = 'CONSULTAR' And Not _Flag
		Loop
	Endif

	Store '' To lcOrigem, lcDestino

	If Inlist( _PuxaOrigem, 1, 2 )
		If _PuxaOrigem = 1
			If oPendSnope.CkAtCta = 1 And !IsEmpty( CrEest.ContaOs )
				lcOrigem = CrEest.Grupoos + '/' + CrEest.ContaOs
			Else
				If crSigOpCdi.OrigDest = 1 And !IsEmpty( TprMvCab.ContaOs )
					If TprMvCab.ContaOs # CrEest.ContaOs And TprMvCab.ContaOs # CrEest.ContaDs
						Loop
					EndIf 
				Else
					If !IsEmpty( TprMvCab.ContaOs ) And TprMvCab.ContaOs # CrEest.ContaOs
						Loop
					Endif
				EndIf 
				lcOrigem = CrEest.Grupoos + '/' + CrEest.ContaOs
			EndIf 
		Else
			If oPendSnope.CkAtCta = 1 And !IsEmpty( CrEest.contads )
				lcOrigem = CrEest.Grupods + '/' + CrEest.contads
			Else
				If crSigOpCdi.OrigDest = 1 And !IsEmpty( TprMvCab.ContaOs )
					If TprMvCab.ContaOs # CrEest.ContaOs And TprMvCab.ContaOs # CrEest.ContaDs
						Loop
					EndIf 
				Else
					If !IsEmpty( TprMvCab.ContaOs ) And TprMvCab.ContaOs # CrEest.contads
						Loop
					Endif
				EndIf 
				lcOrigem = CrEest.Grupods + '/' + CrEest.contads
			EndIf 
		Endif
	Else
		lcOrigem = CrEest.Grupoos + '/' + CrEest.ContaOs
	EndIf
	
	If Inlist( _PuxaDestino, 1, 2 )
		If _PuxaDestino = 1
			If !IsEmpty( TprMvCab.contads ) And TprMvCab.contads # CrEest.ContaOs
				Loop
			Endif
			lcDestino = CrEest.Grupoos + '/' + CrEest.ContaOs
		Else
			If !IsEmpty( TprMvCab.contads ) And TprMvCab.contads # CrEest.contads
				Loop
			Endif
			lcDestino = CrEest.Grupods + '/' + CrEest.contads
		Endif
	Else
		lcDestino = CrEest.Grupods + '/' + CrEest.contads
	Endif

	lnChkSubN  = Iif(CrEest.LCancelas,3,Iif(CrEest.ChkSubN,1,Iif(CrEest.ChkBxParcs,2,0)))
	lcDescO = Space(40)
	lcDescD = Space(40)
	If !Empty(CrEest.ContaOs)
		If Not Seek(CrEest.ContaOs,'CrContas','Iclis')
			lcQuery = [Select Iclis, Rclis From SigCdCli Where Iclis = "]+CrEest.ContaOs+["]
			If (Thisform.parentform.podataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpCli') < 1)
				=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpcli - 1)')
				Thisform.LiberaTimer = .T.
				Return 0
			Endif
			Select CrContas
			Append Blank
			Replace Iclis With CrTmpcli.Iclis,;
				Rclis With CrTmpcli.Rclis In CrContas
		Endif
		lcDescO = CrContas.Rclis
	Endif
	If !Empty(CrEest.contads)
		If Not Seek(CrEest.contads,'CrContas','Iclis')
			lcQuery = [Select Iclis, Rclis From SigCdCli Where Iclis = "]+CrEest.contads+["]
			If (Thisform.parentform.podataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpCli') < 1)
				=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpcli - 2)')
				Thisform.LiberaTimer = .T.
				Return 0
			Endif
			Select CrContas
			Append Blank
			Replace Iclis With CrTmpcli.Iclis,;
				Rclis With CrTmpcli.Rclis In CrContas
		Endif
		lcDescD = CrContas.Rclis
	Endif

	m.Valos = CrEest.Valos

	lcSql = [Select * From SigCdTif Where Codigos = ']+CrEest.TpFats+[']
	If Thisform.parentform.podataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrSigCdTif') < 1
		=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão carregar (crSigCdTif)')
		Thisform.LiberaTimer = .T.
		Return 0
	Endif
	If Inlist(crSigCdOpe.ApTpFats,1,4) And !IsEmpty( CrEest.TpFats ) And CrSigCdTif.pValors # 0
		If crSigCdOpe.ApTpFats = 1
			If crSigCdOpe.NDebitos # 1
				If Inlist( crSigCdOpe.NFiscals, 1, 2 )
					m.Valos = m.Valos * CrSigCdTif.pValors / 100
				Endif
			Else
				If Inlist( crSigCdOpe.NFiscals, 1, 2 )
					m.Valos = m.Valos * CrSigCdTif.pValors / 100
				Else
					If CrSigCdTif.pValors <> 0
						m.Valos = ( m.Valos / CrSigCdTif.pValors * 100 ) * ( 1 - ( CrSigCdTif.pValors / 100 ) )
					Endif
				Endif
			Endif
		Else
			If crSigCdOpe.NDebitos # 1
				If Inlist( crSigCdOpe.NFiscals, 1, 2 )
					m.Valos = m.Valos - ( m.Valos * CrSigCdTif.pValors / 100 )
				Endif
			Else
				If Inlist( crSigCdOpe.NFiscals, 1, 2 )
					m.Valos = m.Valos - ( m.Valos * CrSigCdTif.pValors / 100 )
				Else
					If CrSigCdTif.pValors <> 0
						m.Valos = m.Valos - ( ( m.Valos / CrSigCdTif.pValors * 100 ) * ( 1 - ( CrSigCdTif.pValors / 100 ) ) )
					Endif
				Endif
			Endif
		Endif
	Endif

	If Thisform.ncaritem
		ncodigo = ( ( crTmpOpe.NDopes * 1000000 ) + CrEest.Numes )
		EmpPend = CrEest.Emps
		lcSql = [Select CidChaves From SigMvPec Where Codigos = ]+Str(ncodigo)+[ And Dopes = ']+TprMvCab.dopes+[']+;
			[ And Empsubns = ']+EmpPend+[']
		If Thisform.parentform.podataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'TmpEstPe') < 1
			=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão carregar (TmpEstPe')
			Thisform.LiberaTimer = .T.
			Return 0
		Endif
		If Reccount('TmpEstPe') > 0
			Loop
		Endif
	Endif

	If Inlist(crSigCdOpe.CupFis,1,5,6)		&& mena 27/10/2015 incluido 5 e 6 para mostrar vendedor
		lcOrigem = CrEest.Vends
	Endif

	llIns = .T.
	If (crSigCdOpe.CupFis = 1) And Not (CrSigOpCdc.cnCaixas = [999]) && Cupom Fiscal
		lcQuery = [Select Units ] + ;
			[From SigMvItn ] + ;
			[Where EmpDopNums = '] + CrEest.Emps + CrEest.dopes + Str(CrEest.Numes, 6) + [']

		If (Thisform.parentform.podataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'LocalItens') < 1)
			=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalItens)')
			Thisform.LiberaTimer = .T.
			Return 0
		Endif

		Select LocalItens
		Scan
			lcVal = LocalItens.Units
			lcVal = Alltrim(Str(lcVal, 16, 6))
			lcVal = Alltrim(Substr(lcVal, At([.], lcVal) + 1, 10))

			For I = 3 To Len(lcVal)
				If Not Inlist(Substr(lcVal, I, 1), [ ], [0] )
					llIns = .F.
				Endif
			Next
		Endscan
	Endif

	If llIns
		Insert Into TmpPends (Flag, Emps, dopes, Numes, Mascnum, DataS, Origem, Destino, Valos, ;
			Notas, ChkSubN, Obss, DescsO, DescsD, cIdChaves, Usuars, Empds, pStatus, ;
			DtEnts, TpFats, ObsCabMovs, Jobs, PedidoWeb, IDEntrega, ;
			ChaveNota, IDTransp, NumeroNota, EmpRet) ;
			Values (_Flag, CrEest.Emps, CrEest.dopes, CrEest.Numes, ;
			CrEest.Mascnum, CrEest.DataS, lcOrigem, lcDestino, m.Valos, ;
			CrEest.Notas, lnChkSubN, CrEest.Obses, lcDescO, lcDescD, ;
			fUniqueIds(), CrEest.Usuars, CrEest.Empds, CrEest.pStatus, CrEest.PrazoEnts, ;
			CrEest.TpFats, CrEest.ObsCabMovs, CrEest.Jobs, CrEest.PedidoWeb, crEest.IDEntrega, ;
			crEest.ChaveNota, crEest.IDTransp, crEest.NumeroNota, crEest.EmpRet)
		If !Seek(CrEest.dopes,'TmpDop','Dopes')
			Insert Into Tmpdop ( dopes ) Values ( CrEest.dopes )
		Endif
	Endif
Endscan

Thisform.LimparJobs() && 31/07/2014 - Richard

Thisform.LiberaTimer = .T.
Thisform.Refresh
Return .T.

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE carregar2
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE carregar2
Lparameters pCodigo, pNota

Thisform.LiberaTimer = .f.

xDAteT = Datetime()
DO While Datetime() - xDateT < 2
Enddo

lcSql = [Select * From SigCdOpe Where dopes = ']+TprMvCab.Dopes+[']
If ThisForm.Parentform.PodataMgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrSigCdOpe') < 1
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão carregar (crSigCdOpe)')
	Return 0
EndIf

_DopesNivel = crSigCdOpe.Nivels
_Opera = crSigCdOpe.Opers
_PuxaOrigem = crSigCdOpe.ConOSubs
*** mena 27/10/2015 incluido 5 e 6 
_PuxaDestino = IIF(INLIST(crSigCdOpe.CupFis,1,5,6), 3, crSigCdOpe.ConDSubs)	&& Mena 21/03/2014 para carregar subnivel independente de conta de destino
_SubnB = crSigCdOpe.SubNs=3 And CrSigOpCdc.NCarBxs = 1
llSubnt  = .f.
llPagoT  = .t.
llPagoF  = .f.
llSubntT = .t.
lnDiaPd  = Iif(ThisForm.Validardias,ThisForm.Get_meses.Value,0)
LnMesPd  = Iif(ThisForm.ValidarDias,0,ThisForm.Get_meses.Value)
llDtDD   = fDtoSQL(Date()+1-lnDiaPd)
llDtMM   = fDtoSQL(Gomonth(Date(),LnMesPd*-1))
lcWhr    = [ ]

ThisForm.Caption = 'Operações Pendentes ( * )'
ThisForm.Refresh
Create Cursor CrContas (Iclis c(10), Rclis c(40))
Index On Iclis Tag Iclis

Select TmpPends
Set Order To EmpDopNum

lcDp = Space(20)
Select TmpOperacao
_ct = Reccount()
_ca = 0
Scan
	_ca = _ca + 1
	lcSql = [Select dopes from SigCdOpe Where Ndopes = ]+Str(Val(Left(Str(TmpOperacao.Codigos,10),4)))
	LnErro = ThisForm.ParentForm.PoDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'TmpOpe')
	If lnErro < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (SigCdOpe 10)')
		Return .f.
	Endif
	Dope = TmpOpe.Dopes
	Nume = Val( Right( Str( TmpOperacao.Codigos, 10 ), 6) )
	lcBusca = TmpOperacao.EmpSubns + Dope + Str(Nume,6)

	lcquery = [Select Emps, dopes, Numes, GrupoOs, ContaOs, GrupoDs, ContaDs, lCancelas, ChkSubn, ChkBxParcs, ]+;
			  [MascNum, Datas, Valos, Notas, Obses, TpFats, CodTrans, LocalEnts, Usuars, EmpDs, pStatus, PrazoEnts, Vends, NumeroNota, EmpRet ] + ;
			  [From SigMvCab Where ]+;
			  [Empdopnums = ']+lcBusca+[']

	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrEest') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (xcreest)')
		Thisform.LiberaTimer = .t.
		Return 0
	EndIf
		
	If CrEest.Dopes # lcDp
		lcDp = CrEest.Dopes
		lcSql = [Select Dopes,nDopes From SigCdOpe Where dopes = ']+lcDp+[']
		If ThisForm.Parentform.PodataMgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrTmpOpe') < 1
			=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão carregar (crSigCdOpe)')
			Thisform.LiberaTimer = .t.
			Return 0
		EndIf
	EndIf

	Store '' to lcOrigem, lcDestino
	
	If Inlist( _PuxaOrigem, 1, 2 )
		If _PuxaOrigem = 1
			If !IsEmpty( TprMvCab.Contaos ) And TprMvCab.Contaos # crEest.Contaos
				Loop
			Endif
			lcOrigem = crEest.Grupoos + '/' + crEest.Contaos
		Else
			If !IsEmpty( TprMvCab.Contaos ) And TprMvCab.Contaos # crEest.Contads
				Loop
			Endif
			lcOrigem = crEest.Grupods + '/' + crEest.Contads
		Endif
	Else
		lcOrigem = crEest.Grupoos + '/' + crEest.Contaos
	Endif
	If Inlist( _PuxaDestino, 1, 2 )
		If _PuxaDestino = 1
			If !IsEmpty( TprMvCab.Contads ) And TprMvCab.Contads # crEest.Contaos
				Loop
			Endif
			lcDestino = crEest.Grupoos + '/' + crEest.Contaos
		Else
			If !IsEmpty( TprMvCab.Contads ) And TprMvCab.Contads # crEest.Contads
				Loop
			Endif
			lcDestino = crEest.Grupods + '/' + crEest.Contads
		Endif
	Else
		lcDestino = crEest.Grupods + '/' + crEest.Contads
	Endif

	lnChkSubN  = Iif(crEest.LCancelas,3,Iif(crEest.ChkSubN,1,Iif(crEest.ChkBxParcs,2,0)))
	lcDescO = Space(40)
	lcDescD = Space(40)
	If !Empty(crEest.ContaOs)
		If Not Seek(crEest.ContaOs,'CrContas','Iclis')
			lcQuery = [Select Iclis, Rclis From SigCdCli Where Iclis = "]+CrEest.ContaOs+["]  
			If (ThisForm.parentform.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpCli') < 1)
				=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpcli - 1)')
				Thisform.LiberaTimer = .t.
				Return 0
			EndIf
			Select CrContas
			Append Blank
			Replace Iclis With CrTmpcli.Iclis,;
					Rclis With CrTmpcli.Rclis in CrContas
		Endif
		lcDescO = CrContas.RClis
	EndIf
	If !Empty(crEest.ContaDs)
		If Not Seek(crEest.ContaDs,'CrContas','Iclis')
			lcQuery = [Select Iclis, Rclis From SigCdCli Where Iclis = "]+CrEest.ContaDs+["]
			If (ThisForm.parentform.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpCli') < 1)
				=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpcli - 2)')
				Thisform.LiberaTimer = .t.
				Return 0
			EndIf
			Select CrContas
			Append Blank
			Replace Iclis With CrTmpcli.Iclis,;
					Rclis With CrTmpcli.Rclis in CrContas
		Endif
		lcDescD = crContas.RClis
	EndIf

	m.Valos = crEest.Valos
	
	lcSql = [Select * From SigCdTif Where Codigos = ']+CrEest.TpFats+[']
	If ThisForm.Parentform.PodataMgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrSigCdTif') < 1
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão carregar (crSigCdTif)')
		Thisform.LiberaTimer = .t.
		Return 0
	EndIf
	If InList(CrSigCdOpe.ApTpFats,1,4) And !IsEmpty( CrEest.TpFats ) And CrSigCdTif.pValors # 0
		If CrSigCdOpe.ApTpFats = 1
			If crSigCdOpe.NDebitos # 1
				If Inlist( crSigCdOpe.NFiscals, 1, 2 )
					m.Valos = m.Valos * crSigCdTif.PValors / 100
				Endif
			Else
				If Inlist( crSigCdOpe.NFiscals, 1, 2 )
					m.Valos = m.Valos * crSigCdTif.PValors / 100
				Else
					If CrSigCdTif.PValors <> 0
						m.Valos = ( m.Valos / crSigCdTif.PValors * 100 ) * ( 1 - ( crSigCdTif.PValors / 100 ) ) 
					Endif
				Endif
			EndIf
		Else
			If crSigCdOpe.NDebitos # 1
				If Inlist( crSigCdOpe.NFiscals, 1, 2 )
					m.Valos = m.Valos - ( m.Valos * crSigCdTif.PValors / 100 )
				Endif
			Else
				If Inlist( crSigCdOpe.NFiscals, 1, 2 )
					m.Valos = m.Valos - ( m.Valos * crSigCdTif.PValors / 100 )
				Else
					If CrSigCdTif.PValors <> 0
						m.Valos = m.Valos - ( ( m.Valos / crSigCdTif.PValors * 100 ) * ( 1 - ( crSigCdTif.PValors / 100 ) ) )
					EndIf
				Endif
			EndIf
		Endif
	EndIf

	If INLIST(crSigCdOpe.CupFis,1,5,6)	&& mena 27/10/2015 incluido 5 e 6 para mostrar vendedor
		lcOrigem = crEest.Vends
	EndIf
	
	llIns = .t.
	If (crSigCdOpe.CupFis = 1) And Not (crSigOpCdc.cnCaixas = [999]) && Cupom Fiscal
		lcquery = [Select Units ] + ;
					[From SigMvItn ] + ;
				   [Where EmpDopNums = '] + crEest.Emps + crEest.Dopes + Str(crEest.Numes, 6) + [']

		If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'LocalItens') < 1)
			=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalItens)')
			Thisform.LiberaTimer = .t.
			Return 0
		EndIf
		
		Select LocalItens
		Scan
			lcVal = LocalItens.Units
			lcVal = Alltrim(Str(lcVal, 16, 6))
			lcVal = Alltrim(Substr(lcVal, At([.], lcVal) + 1, 10))

			For I = 3 To Len(lcVal)
				If Not InList(Substr(lcVal, I, 1), [ ], [0] )
					llIns = .f.
				EndIf
			Next
		EndScan
	EndIf
	
	If llIns	
		Insert Into TmpPends ( Flag, Emps, Dopes, Numes, Mascnum, Datas, Origem, Destino, Valos, ;
				Notas, ChkSubN, Obss, DescsO, DescsD, cIdChaves, Usuars, EmpDs, pStatus, DtEnts,TpFats, ;
				NumeroNota, EmpRet ) ;
				Values ( .t., creest.Emps, creest.Dopes, creest.Numes, ;
				creest.MascNum, creest.Datas, lcOrigem, lcDestino, m.Valos, ;
				crEest.Notas, lnChkSubn, crEest.Obses, lcDescO, lcDescD, ;
				fUniqueIds(), crEest.Usuars, CrEest.EmpDs, CrEest.pStatus, CrEest.PrazoEnts, CrEest.TpFats, ;
				crEest.NumeroNota, crEest.EmpRet )
		If !Seek(CrEest.Dopes,'TmpDop','Dopes')
			Insert Into Tmpdop ( Dopes ) Values ( CrEest.Dopes )
		EndIf 
	EndIf 
EndScan
Thisform.LiberaTimer = .t.
ThisForm.Refresh
Return .t.

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE desmarca
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE desmarca
Local _PuxaValVars, _nItens, lnOldCItens

ThisForm.parentform.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdOpe','CrSigCdOpe','dopes',TprMvCab.Dopes)

ThisForm.parentform.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdOpe','TmpCdOpe','dopes',TprMvCab.Dopes)

_PuxaValVars = crSigCdOpe.Vars
_BaixaSubN   = ( crSigCdOpe.BaixaSns = 1 )
_BxParc      = ( crSigCdOpe.BxParcials = 1 )
_BxAutos     = ( crSigCdOpe.BxAutos = 1 )
_PuxaOrigem  = crSigCdOpe.ConOSubs
_PuxaDestino = crSigCdOpe.ConDSubs
_CarOpCr     = CrSigCdOpe.Opcrs
_pCupFis     = ( Type('ThisForm.pforms') = "O" )

ThisForm.parentform.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdOpe','CrSigCdOpe','dopes',TmpPends.Dopes)
ThisForm.parentform.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpCdc','CrSigOpCdc','dopes',TmpPends.Dopes)

_Nivel = TmpPends.Emps + TmpPends.Dopes + Str( TmpPends.Numes, 6 )
_NumNivel = Val( Str( crSigCdOpe.Ndopes, 4 ) + Transf( TmpPends.Numes, '@L 999999' ) )

Select crTpmMvItn
Delete All for Dopes + Str( Numes, 6 ) = TmpPends.Dopes + Str( TmpPends.Numes, 6 )

Select crTpmMvItnS
Delete All for Dopes + Str( Numes, 6 ) = TmpPends.Dopes + Str( TmpPends.Numes, 6 )

Select xBaixaSS
Delete All for EmpDopNums = TmpPends.EmpSubns + TmpPends.Dopes + Str( TmpPends.Numes, 6 )

Select crTpmMvIts
Set Order to CItens
Set Key to
Delete All for Dopes + Str( Numes, 6 ) = TmpPends.Dopes + Str( TmpPends.Numes, 6 )

Select crSigSubMv
Set Order to CItem2
Set Key to
Delete All for Dopes + Str( Numes, 6 ) = TmpPends.Dopes + Str( TmpPends.Numes, 6 )

Select crSigMvOrc
Set Order to CItens
Set Key to
Delete All for Dopes + Str( Numes, 6 ) = TmpPends.Dopes + Str( TmpPends.Numes, 6 )

Select crSigMvOrp
Set Order to CItens
Set Key to
Delete All for Dopes + Str( Numes, 6 ) = TmpPends.Dopes + Str( TmpPends.Numes, 6 )

Select xPar
Delete all for Dopes + Str( Numes, 6 ) = TmpPends.Dopes + Str( TmpPends.Numes, 6 )

Select TprMvCabV
Delete all for Dopes + Str( Numes, 6 ) = TmpPends.Dopes + Str( TmpPends.Numes, 6 )

If Type('ThisForm.pforms') = "O"
	Select xParTef
	Delete All For EmpDopNums = TmpPends.Emps + TmpPends.Dopes + Str(TmpPends.Numes,6)
EndIf

Select TmpOperacao
If Seek( TmpPends.Emps + Str(_NumNivel,10), 'TmpOperacao', 'Codigos' )
	Delete
EndIf

ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigMvCab', 'CrTprMvCab', 'EmpDopNums', _nivel)
Go Top In CrTprMvCab

If _CarOpCr = 6
	Select TmpDevol
	Delete all for Dopes + Str( Numes, 6 ) = TmpPends.Dopes + Str( TmpPends.Numes, 6 )
Endif

ThisForm.submarcado = ThisForm.SubMarcado - 1
If Thisform.SubMarcado > 0
	ThisForm.Get_Meses.Enabled = .f.
Else
	ThisForm.Get_Meses.Enabled = .t.
Endif

llSubn = .f.
llDtAlt = Datetime()
llDtBx = Null
If _BaixaSubn
	lcUpDate = [Update SigMvCab Set ChkSubn = ?llSubn, DtAlts = ?llDtAlt, DtBaixas = ?llDtBx Where EmpDopNums = ']+_Nivel+[']
	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão ')
		Return .f.
	EndIf
Else
	If Not ThisForm.Parentform.podatamgr.chkregister('SigBxEst','EmpDopNumb',_Nivel)
		lcUpDate = [Update SigMvCab Set ChkBxParcs = ?llSubn, DtAlts = ?llDtAlt Where EmpDopNums = ']+_Nivel+[']
		If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'') < 1)
			=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão ')
			Return .f.
		EndIf
	Endif
Endif

*** Tiago - 01/07/2015 - Incluído para atualizar o status do subnível quando desmarca
If Not Empty(crSigOpCdd.StsFixs) And (Int(Val(Substr(crSigOpCdi.lbltxidCs, 16, 1))) <> 2) && 1-Subnível; 2-Movimentação Atual
	Select crStsMvPec
	Go Top
	Locate For Codigos = _NumNivel
	lcUpDate = [Update SigMvCab Set pStatus = '] + crStsMvPec.Pstatus + [' Where EmpDopNums = ']+_Nivel+[']
	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Registro em Uso por Outro Usuario', 16, 'Falha na Conexão ')
		Return .f.
	Endif
	*** Tiago - 01/07/2015 - Zenith - Deleta do cursor para não atualizar os subníveis desmarcados
	Select crStsMvPec	
	Delete For Codigos = _NumNivel
Endif

Select TprMvCab
If TprMvCab.Espes = CrTprMvCab.Espes
	Replace TprMvCab.Qtdes with TprMvCab.Qtdes - CrTprMvCab.Qtdes
Endif

Replace TprMvCab.PLiqs with TprMvCab.PLiqs - CrTprMvCab.PLiqs,;
		TprMvCab.PBrus with TprMvCab.PBrus - CrTprMvCab.PBrus
	
If _PuxaValVars = 1
	Replace TprMvCab.ValVars With TprMvCab.ValVars - crTprMvCab.ValVars
	If CrSigOpCdd.VarPs = 1 And ;
			( (_pCupFis And CrSigCdOpe.ParAutos = 1 ) Or (Not _pCupFis And InList(CrSigCdOpe.ParAutos,1,4,5)) )
		Replace ValVarPs With ValVarPs - CrTprMvCab.ValVarPs in TprMvCab
	Endif
EndIf

If InList( _PuxaOrigem, 1, 2 ) And ThisForm.Submarcado = 0
	If !IsEmpty( TprMvCab.Grupoos ) And !crSigCdOpe.FixGOrigs
		Replace TprMvCab.Grupoos with Space(10)
	Endif
	If !IsEmpty( TprMvCab.Contaos ) 
		Replace TprMvCab.Contaos with Space(10)
	Endif
Endif

If InList( _PuxaDestino, 1, 2 ) And ThisForm.SubMarcado = 0
	If !IsEmpty( TprMvCab.Grupods ) And !crSigCdOpe.FixGDests
		Replace TprMvCab.Grupods with Space(10)
	Endif
	If !IsEmpty( TprMvCab.Contads ) 
		Replace TprMvCab.Contads with Space(10)
	Endif
Endif

*** mena 30/11/2015 - se vends = usuario, não limpa pois já foi carregado antes independe do subnivel ***
If !IsEmpty( TprMvCab.Vends ) and crSigCdOpe.vendes <> 4 And ThisForm.SubMarcado = 0
	Replace TprMvCab.Vends With Space(10)
Endif
		
If !IsEmpty( TprMvCab.Resps ) And ThisForm.SubMarcado = 0
	Replace TprMvCab.Resps With Space(10)
EndIf

If !IsEmpty( TprMvCab.EmpDs )

*!*		If !(crSigCdOpe.Confes # 1 And crSigOpCdc.CarESubns <> 1)
*!*			Replace TprMvCab.EmpDs With Iif(Not Empty(crSigCdOpe.EmpPads),crSigCdOpe.EmpPads,Space(3)) 
*!*		EndIf 

	Replace TprMvCab.EmpDs With [] in TprMvCab
	If Not Empty(TmpCdOpe.EmpPads)
		Replace TprMvCab.EmpDs With TmpCdOpe.EmpPads in TprMvCab
	EndIf 
EndIf 

If !IsEmpty( TprMvCab.nPedClis ) 
	Replace nPedClis With 0
EndIf 

If !IsEmpty( TprMvCab.PedidoWeb ) 
	Replace PedidoWeb With []
EndIf 

If !IsEmpty( TprMvCab.IDEntrega ) 
	Replace IDEntrega With []
EndIf 

If !IsEmpty( TprMvCab.CDRastreio ) 
	Replace CDRastreio With []
EndIf 

Select crTpmMvIts
Set Order to
Select crSigSubMv
Set Order To
_nItens = 0
Select crTpmMvItn
Scan
	_nItens = _nItens - 1
	lnOldCItens = crTpmMvItn.CItens
	Replace Citens With _nItens
	Select crTpmMvIts
	Replace All CItens With _nItens For (Emps+Dopes+Str(Numes,6)+Cpros = crTpmMvItn.Emps+crTpmMvItn.Dopes+Str(crTpmMvItn.Numes,6)+crTpmMvItn.Cpros) And crTpmMvIts.CItens = lnOldCItens
	Select crSigSubMv
	Replace All CItem2 With _nItens For (Emps+Dopes+Str(Numes,6)+Cpros = crTpmMvItn.Emps+crTpmMvItn.Dopes+Str(crTpmMvItn.Numes,6)+crTpmMvItn.Cpros) And crSigSubMv.CItem2 = lnOldCItens
	Select crTpmMvItn
Endscan

Select crTpmMvItn
Replace All CItens With Abs(CItens)
Goto Top

Select crTpmMvIts
Replace All CItens With Abs(CItens)
Set Order to CItens
Set Key to crTpmMvItn.CItens		

Select crSigSubMv
Replace All CItem2 With Abs(CItem2)
Set Order to CItem2

*!*	*** Tiago - 11/07/2012 - Ícaro - Unlock o registro que foi desmarcado
*!*	CidChav = CrTprMvCab.CidChaves
*!*	ThisForm.parentform.Podatamgr.SqlExecute([Update SigMvCab set lock = null where CidChaves = '] + CidChav + ['],'')

ThisForm.parentform.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdOpe','CrSigCdOpe','dopes',TprMvCab.Dopes)

Return

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE desmembra
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE desmembra
LParameters _Valor, _DesmembraItem1

Local Desmemb, _RecNo

If _DesmembraItem1
	Select crTpmMvItn
	Desmemb=.t.
	If (_Valor+Thisform.citem-1) > CrSigCdOpe.qtdites
		If MessageBox( 'Quantidade Maior Quer a Máxima Permitida na Movimentação !!!' + ;
					   Chr(13) + Chr(10) + 'Deseja Continuar Com o Valor de ' + ;
					   Str( ( CrSigCdOpe.QtdItes - Thisform.CItem + 1 ), 3 ) + '?',4+256+32)=6
			_Valor = CrSigCdOpe.QtdItes - Thisform.CItem + 1
			Replace crTpmMvItn.qtds with _Valor in crTpmMvItn
		Else
			Replace crTpmMvItn.Qtds with 1 in crTpmMvItn
			Desmemb = .f.
		Endif
	Endif
	
	If Desmemb
		Select crTpmMvItn
		_RecNo = Recno('crTpmMvItn')
		Scatter Memvar
		m.Qtds = 1
		m.cidchaves = fUniqueIds()
		Wait Window 'Aguarde... Desmembrando Itens!!!' Nowait
		Do While _Valor > 1
			Thisform.CItem = Thisform.CItem + 1
			m.CItens = ThisForm.CItem
			Insert Into crTpmMvItn From Memvar
			_Valor = _Valor - 1
		EndDo
		Wait clear
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Locate For Recno() = _RecNo
		Replace Qtds With _Valor in crTpmMvItn
		Delete For Empty(Cpros)
		Scan
			If DesMembs
				Loop
			EndIf
			Replace DesMembs With .t. in crTpmMvItn
			=ThisForm.ParentForm.Calc_ValIte()
		EndScan
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Return .t.
	Else
		Return .f.	
	Endif
Else
	*** Tiago - 01/06/2011
*!*		Select crTpmMvItn
*!*		Locate for .f.
*!*		nreg = Recno('crTpmMvItn')
	lnCitens = thisform.citem
	***	
	lnQtds = _Valor - 1
	Select crTpmMvIts
	Replace crTpmMvIts.Qtds With 1

	*** Tiago - 01/06/2011		
	Select crTpmMvItn
	Go top
	Locate for cItens = crTpmMvIts.cItens
	If !Eof()
		replace Codcors with crTpmMvIts.CodCors in crTpmMvItn
		replace CodTams with crTpmMvIts.CodTams in crTpmMvItn
	EndIf
	***
	
	Select crTpmMvIts
	Scatter MemVar
	m.Qtds = 1
	Do While lnQtds > 0
		lnCitens = lnCitens - 1
		m.cItens = lnCitens
		Insert Into crTpmMvIts From MemVar
		lnQtds = lnQtds - 1

		*** Tiago - 01/06/2011		
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Go top
		Locate for cItens = crTpmMvIts.cItens
		If !Eof()
			replace Codcors with crTpmMvIts.CodCors in crTpmMvItn
			replace CodTams with crTpmMvIts.CodTams in crTpmMvItn
		EndIf
		***
	EndDo

	*** Tiago - 01/06/2011		
*!*		Select crTpmMvItn
*!*		Go nReg
	***	
	Select crTpmMvIts
	Return
Endif

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE desmembrac
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE desmembrac
Lparameters _Produto

Local _RecNo, Desmemb, Oper, OpePad, _cItem2, llRetorno, _Ordem

Oper       = CrSigCdOpe.Opers
OpePad     = CrSigCdOpe.OpePads
OpePadC    = CrSigOpCdd.OpePadcs
llRetorno  = .T.

Desmemb=.T.
Select crTpmMvItn
_RecNo = Recno('crTpmMvItn')
_QtdsP = crTpmMvItn.Qtds && Tiago - 01/10/2013 - Incluído para multiplicar a qtde do produto nos componentes
Scatter Memvar
_cItem2 = m.cItens

*** Tiago - 01/10/13 - Se alguma composição tem COR ou TAMANHO, exclui o crTpmMvIts correspondente
Select cItens From crTpmMvItn Where cItem2 = _cItem2 Into Cursor xDelIte2
Select xDelIte2
Scan
	Select crTpmMvIts
	Set Order To
	Set Order To cItens
	Seek ( xDelIte2.cItens )
	Do While Not Eof('crTpmMvIts') And crTpmMvIts.cItens = xDelIte2.cItens
		Delete
		Seek ( xDelIte2.cItens )
	Enddo
Endscan
***

Select crTpmMvItn
Delete For cItem2 = m.cItens
Cont = 0
Scan
	Cont = Cont + 1
	Select crTpmMvIts
	Set Order To
	Set Order To cItens
	Seek crTpmMvItn.cItens
	Scan While cItens = crTpmMvItn.cItens
		Replace cItens With Cont
	Endscan

	Select crSigSubMv
	Set Order To
	Set Order To cItem2
	Seek crTpmMvItn.cItens
	Scan While cItem2 = crTpmMvItn.cItens
		Replace cItem2 With Cont
	Endscan

	Select crTpmMvItn
	_cIt = cItens
	_RecNo2 = Recno()
	Replace cItem2 With Cont For cItem2 = _cIt
	Go _RecNo2
	Replace cItens With Cont
Endscan
Thisform.CItem = Cont

llPes = .T.
lcQuery = [Select lbltxidCs ] + ;
	[From SigOpCdi ] + ;
	[Where Dopes = '] + CrSigCdOpe.Dopes + [']

If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [TmpOpCdi]) < 1)
	=Messagebox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (TmpOpCdi)])
	Return .F.
Endif
Go Top In TmpOpCdi
If (Substr(TmpOpCdi.lbltxidCs, 3, 1) = [1]) && Não Carrega Peso do Subnível
	llPes = .F.
Endif

_Ordem = Iif(crSigOpCdc.CarCompos=7,[ And Ordts = 1 ],Iif(Inlist(crSigOpCdc.CarCompos,8,9),[ And Ordts = 0 ],[]))	&& mena 22/03/2017 - inclui ordem 0
Use In TmpOpCdi
lcSql = [Select Mats, Qtds, Pesos, Pcompos From SigPrCpo Where Cpros = ']+_Produto+[']+_Ordem+[ Order by Mats]
If Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrTmpCompo') > 0
	Select CrTmpCompo
	Scan
		Wait Window 'Aguarde... Carregando Itens de Composicao !!!' Nowait

		lcQuerPro = [Select a.Cpros, a.Cgrus, a.Reffs, a.Situas, a.Cunis, a.CUniPs, a.Dpros, a.Pesoms, a.fvendas, a.fcustos, a.moepvs, ]+;
			[a.moepcs, a.moevs, a.pvens, a.moecs, a.pcuss, a.moedas, a.valors, a.cbars, a.linhas, a.colecoes, ]+;
			[a.mUltComps, a.vUltComps, a.cftios, a.Sgrus, a.mercs, a.CodFinP, a.LtMinsv, b.nTipoJoals, a.custofs ]+;
			[From SigCdPro a Inner Join SigCdGrp b on b.cGrus = a.cGrus Where a.Cpros = ']+CrTmpCompo.Mats+[']

		=Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuerPro,'CrTmpPro')

		If Not Empty(CrSigOpCdd.Mercs) And CrSigOpCdd.Mercs <> CrTmpPro.Mercs
			Loop
		Endif

		If Not llPes And (CrTmpPro.nTipoJoals = 6)
			Replace Pesos With 0 In CrTmpCompo
		Endif

		lcSql = [Select Cgrus From SigOpGpo Where dopes = ']+TprMvCab.Dopes+[']
		=Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'TmpgpOpe')
		Select tmpgpOpe
		Index On Cgrus Tag Cgrus
		llTemGru = Reccount('TmpgpOpe') > 0

		If llTemGru And Not Seek(CrTmpPro.Cgrus,'TmpGpOpe','Cgrus')
			Loop
		Endif

		Select crTpmMvItn
		Set Order To CPros

		*-- Rafael - 02/03/2018 - Agrupa referencias
		llAgrupa = (crSigOpCdd.AgrCompo = 1)
		If llAgrupa
			If Seek(CrTmpCompo.Mats,[crTpmMvItn],[CPros])
				m.cItens = crTpmMvItn.cItens
				m.cItem2 = crTpmMvItn.cItem2
				
				Select crTpmMvItn
				Set Order To
				Replace Qtds   With Qtds + CrTmpCompo.Qtds * _QtdsP,;
						Pesos  With Pesos + CrTmpCompo.Pesos * _QtdsP
			Else
				llAgrupa = .F.
			EndIf 
		EndIf
		
		Select crTpmMvItn
		If Not llAgrupa
			Seek Space(14)
			If Eof()
				Append Blank
				Thisform.CItem = Thisform.CItem + 1
				m.cItens = Thisform.CItem
			Else
				m.cItens = crTpmMvItn.cItens
			Endif

			Select crTpmMvItn
			Set Order To
			Replace cItens With m.cItens,;
				CPros  With CrTmpCompo.Mats,;
				Qtds   With CrTmpCompo.Qtds * _QtdsP,;
				Pesos  With CrTmpCompo.Pesos * _QtdsP,;
				cItem2 With _cItem2
		EndIf 

		If Inlist(CrSigCdOpe.PrecoAuts,1,3)
			lcSql = [Select Codigos from SigCdLpc Where lPrecos = ']+TprMvCab.lPrecos+[']
			lnErro = Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrTmplPre')
			If lnErro < 1
				Messagebox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return 0
			Endif
			lcwhere = [Select * From SigCdLpi Where Cpros = ']+CrTmpPro.CPros+[' And LPrecos = ']+TprMvCab.lPrecos+[']
			lnErro = Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcwhere,'CrSigCdLpi')
			If lnErro < 1
				Messagebox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return 0
			Endif
			Select CrSigCdLpi
			Go Top
			If Reccount() > 0 And (IsEmpty(CrSigCdLpi.VencFs) Or Between(TprMvCab.DataS,CrSigCdLpi.VencIs, CrSigCdLpi.VencFs))
				lcMoeVs = CrSigCdLpi.MoeVs
				Replace crTpmMvItn.univals With CrSigCdLpi.PVens, ;
					crTpmMvItn.MoeFats   With CrSigCdLpi.MoeVs, ;
					crTpmMvItn.lPrecos   With TprMvCab.lPrecos,;
					crTpmMvItn.CodlPrecs With CrTmplPre.Codigos In crTpmMvItn
			Else
				If CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 1
					lcMoeVs   = CrTmpPro.MoeVs
					lnUniVals = CrTmpPro.PVens
				Else
					If CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 2
						lcMoeVs   = CrTmpPro.MoeCs
						lnUniVals = CrTmpPro.PCuss
						If Inlist(CrSigOpCdd.chkIfors,1,2)
							_Ct = Iif(CrSigOpCdd.chkIfors=1,TprMvCab.ContaOs,TprMvCab.Contads)
							lcQuery = [Select Moevs, Pvens From SigPrFnc Where Cpros = ']+CrTmpPro.CPros+[' And Ifors = ']+_Ct+[']
							=Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpFpro')
							If Not Eof('CrTmpfPro') And CrTmpfPro.PVens > 0 And Not Empty(CrTmpfPro.MoeVs)
								lcMoeVs   = CrTmpfPro.MoeVs
								lnUniVals = CrTmpfPro.PVens
							Endif
						Endif
						lcQuery = [Select MoeQs, QtdEqs ] + ;
							[From SigCdMoe ] + ;
							[Where CMoes = '] + lcMoeVs + [' ] + ;
							[Order By MoeQs, QtdEqs]

						If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpMoe') < 1)
							=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpMoe)')
							Return 0
						Endif
						Go Top In crTmpMoe

						If Not Eof('crTmpMoe')
							If Not IsEmpty(crTmpMoe.Moeqs)
								lcMoeVs   = crTmpMoe.Moeqs
								lnUniVals = lnUniVals * Iif( crTmpMoe.Qtdeqs = 0, 1, crTmpMoe.Qtdeqs )
							Endif
						Endif
					Else
						If CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 5
							lcMoeVs   = CrTmpPro.Moedas
							lnUniVals = CrTmpPro.Valors
						Else
							If CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 7
								lcMoeVs   = CrTmpPro.mUltComps
								lnUniVals = CrTmpPro.vUltComps
							Else
								If Inlist(CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs,8,9)
									=fCustoMedio(TprMvCab.Emps,CrTmpPro.CPros,TprMvCab.DataS,Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr,(CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs=8))
									lcMoeVs   = TmpCusMed.Moedas
									lnUniVals = TmpCusMed.Valors
								Else
									lnUniVals = 0
									lcMoeVs   = CrSigCdOpe.CMoes
								Endif
							Endif
						Endif
					Endif
				Endif
				If CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 11
					lcMoeVs   = CrTmpPro.MoeCs
					lnUniVals = CrTmpPro.custofs
				Endif
				Select crTpmMvItn
				Replace crTpmMvItn.univals With lnUniVals
			Endif
		Else
			lcMoeVs = CrSigCdOpe.CMoes
		Endif

		xOper = Iif( Oper=1, 'E', Iif( Oper = 2, 'S', Iif( Oper = 3,Iif(Not Empty(OpePadC),OpePadC,OpePad), crTpmMvItn.Opers)))

		Select crTpmMvItn
		Replace crTpmMvItn.dpros  With CrTmpPro.dpros, ;
			crTpmMvItn.cunis  With CrTmpPro.cunis, ;
			crTpmMvItn.cunips With CrTmpPro.cunips, ;
			crTpmMvItn.MoeVs  With lcMoeVs,;
			crTpmMvItn.Reffs  With CrTmpPro.Reffs,;
			crTpmMvItn.Opers  With xOper, ;
			crTpmMvItn.CarCompos With Iif(crSigOpCdc.CarCompos=5,.T.,.F.)

		If Inlist(crSigOpCdc.CarCompos,7,8,9)
			Replace crTpmMvItn.univals With CrTmpCompo.pcompos		&& mena 23/03/2017 - carregar preco de custo da composicao
		Endif

		If( CrTmpPro.nTipoJoals == 3 ) And Seek(_cItem2,[crTpmMvIts],[CItens]) And (Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvIts.CodCors) Or Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvIts.CodTams))
			_Cor = crTpmMvIts.CodCors
			_Tam = crTpmMvIts.CodTams
			Select crTpmMvIts
			Append Blank In crTpmMvIts
			Replace cItens  With crTpmMvItn.cItens,;
				Emps    With TprMvCab.Emps,;
				Dopes   With TprMvCab.Dopes,;
				Numes   With TprMvCab.Numes,;
				CPros   With crTpmMvItn.CPros,;
				Qtds    With crTpmMvItn.Qtds,;
				Pesos   With crTpmMvItn.Pesos,;
				QtdEmbs	With 1,;
				CodCors With _Cor,;
				CodTams With _Tam In crTpmMvIts
		Endif
		Select crTpmMvItn

		Thisform.ParentForm.Calc_ValIte()

	Endscan
	llRetorno = .T.
Else
	llRetorno = .F.
Endif
Return llRetorno
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE limparjobs
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE limparjobs
If (Int(Val(Substr(crSigOpCdd.ConfigGers, 160, 01))) = 1)  && Pasta Job / Filtrar Job por Usuário (Sim / Não)
	lcQuery = [Select * ] + ;
			    [From SigClJob ] + ;
			   [Where Usuars = '] + Usuar + [']

	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalClJob]) < 1)
		=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (LocalClJob)])
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	Select LocalClJob
	Go Top In LocalClJob
	If Not Eof([LocalClJob])
		Scan
			Update TmpPends Set Apagar = 1 Where Jobs = LocalClJob.Jobs
		EndScan
		Delete From TmpPends Where Apagar = 0
	EndIf
	Use In LocalClJob
EndIf

* Richard em 03/09/2014 - Eliminar JOBs de Acesso Restrito da Listagem
Update TmpPends Set Apagar = 1
Select TmpPends
Scan
	If Not fChecaAcessoJOB(Usuar, TmpPends.Jobs, ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr)
		Replace Apagar With 0 In TmpPends
	EndIf
EndScan
Delete From TmpPends Where Apagar = 0
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE marca
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE marca
Parameters _Automatico, _MarcaAuto
Private pDtO, pObO

Local lnOldCItens, _PuxaValVars, _CfoSubN, _CupFisSubN, lnValIpi, lnValLbOpe, _CarItens, _SemItens, ;
		_BxAutos, lnCasas, wRegistros, _ncodigo, _ChkEmpDestino, _nddVal, lcMoePend, _CfoAtual, QtdSub, UnitSub, _cpros

lcSql = [Select a.Dopes,a.Cfos,a.CupFis,a.nDopes,a.Vars,b.nDdVals,a.parcs,a.cMoes,b.InibmVends,a.Valpres,a.cadprods, ]+;
		[a.ChkQtdss,a.DigItens From SigCdOpe a, SigOpCdc b ]+;
		[Where a.Dopes = ']+TmpPends.Dopes+[' And a.Dopes = b.Dopes ]
If Thisform.ParentForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrSigCdOpe') < 1
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrSigCdOpe)')
	Return .F.
EndIf

_NivelDopes  = TmpPends.Dopes
_Nivel       = TmpPends.Emps + TmpPends.Dopes + Str( TmpPends.Numes, 6 )
_CfoSubN     = crSigCdOpe.Cfos
_CupFisSubN  = crSigCdOpe.CupFis
_NumNivel    = Val( Str( crSigCdOpe.Ndopes, 4 ) + Transf( TmpPends.Numes, '@L 999999' ) )
_PuxaValVars = crSigCdOpe.Vars
_ncodigo     = ( ( CrSigCdOpe.Ndopes * 1000000 ) + TmpPends.Numes )
_EmpPends    = TmpPends.Emps
_nDdVal      = CrSigCdOpe.nddVals
lcMoePend    = CrSigCdOpe.Cmoes
_pCupFis     = ( Type('ThisForm.pforms') = "O" )
_SemItens    = ( CrSigCdOpe.digitens = 2 )
lcobs = TprMvCab.Obses

_MoeOpe = Iif( Empty( crSigCdOpe.Cmoes ), crSigCdPam.MoedaPs, ;
		  Iif( crSigCdOpe.Valpres=1, crSigCdPam.MoedaPs, crSigCdOpe.CMoes ) )
cotOper = ThisForm.parentForm.Cotacao(_MoeOpe)

** Tiago - 11/09/2015 - Incluído para manter a cotação do subnível
If CrSigOpCdd.AtuCotas = 2
	lcSql = [Select * From SigMvMov Where EmpdopNums = ']+_Nivel+[']
	If Thisform.ParentForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrTmpEstMo') < 1
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpEstmo)')
		Return .F.
	EndIf
	Select CrTmpEstMo
	Scan
		ThisForm.ParentForm.Cotacao( CrTmpEstMo.Moeds, TprMvCab.Datas ) && Insere a cotação do dia no cursor TMPCOT e TMPTOTAL
		If Seek( CrTmpEstMo.Moeds, "TmpCot" )
			Replace Valos With CrTmpEstMo.MoeVals in TmpCot
			ThisForm.ParentForm.Cotacao( CrTmpEstMo.Moeds, TprMvCab.Datas ) && Atualiza o cursor TMPCOT e TMPTOTAL
		Endif
	Endscan
Endif

Thisform.LiberaTimer = .f.
ThisForm.StatusAprRep = ''

If Seek( TmpPends.Emps + Str(_NumNivel,10), 'TmpOperacao', 'Codigos' )
	If !_Automatico
		Messagebox('Movimentação já digitada !!!',0+48,'')
	Endif
	Select TmpPends
	Return .f.
EndIf

*--
=ThisForm.parentForm.PodataMgr.cursorquery('SigOpCdc','CrTmpOpeC','Dopes',TprMvCab.Dopes)
=ThisForm.parentForm.PodataMgr.cursorquery('SigOpCdi','CrTmpOpeI','Dopes',TprMvCab.Dopes)

=ThisForm.parentForm.PodataMgr.cursorquery('SigOpCdd','CrTmpOpe2','Dopes',TprMvCab.Dopes,[ChkLimOps, SubFretes, chklmpss])
**** mena - 08/08/2014 Seleciona registro mais recente da NFe correspondente **** 
If (Thisform.ParentForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute([Select top 1 * From SigPrNfe where empdopnums = '] + _Nivel + [' order by datas desc], [TmpPrNfe]) < 1)
	=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (SigPrNfe)])
	Return .f.
EndIf

lcSql = [Select Opers, Consers, ConsClis, numeras, ItAutos, BaixaSns, BxParcials, BxAutos, ConoSubs, CondSubs, ]+;
		[chDtEntrs, Expends, Casas, Cmoes, TabDescs, ApTpFats, nfiscals, ndebitos, subns, fixgorigs, fixgdests, ]+;
		[lprecos, dtentrs, porcs, arreds, precoauts, precops, subnobrigs, parautos, parrecals, valpres, opepads, ]+;
		[AnProdzs, DigDoc, PedClis, MarcaSubns, Cupfis, QtdPesos, UnitPesos, ePrecos, qtdprecos, ChkDval, carvends, ]+;
		[opcrs, ItSituas, Desmembs, QtdItes, HabSubIts, itemzeros, EditDocs, TabdPs, Vars, Parcs, cMoes, Cfos, ]+;
		[chkAgends,CempSubns,ChecPagos,CadProds, itemalfas, ChkQtdSs, fechmals, ncpesos, NfCompls, Autenticas, ] + ;
		[Dopes, DigItens, InfoIpis, cRepls ]+;
		[From SigCdOpe Where Dopes = ']+TprMvCab.Dopes+[']

If Thisform.ParentForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrSigCdOpe') < 1
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrSigCdOpe 2)')
	Return .F.
EndIf



OpeAtu        = crSigCdOpe.Opers
_Conserto     = crSigCdOpe.Consers = 1
_NumeroAnt    = ( crSigCdOpe.Numeras = 2 )
_DigDocu      = ( CrSigCdOpe.DigDoc = 4 )
_PedClis      = ( crSigCdOpe.PedClis = 5 )
_CarregaItens = InList( crSigCdOpe.ItAutos,1,3,4,5,8,9,10,11 )
_OperItens    = Iif(crSigCdOpe.ItAutos=4,"E",Iif(crSigCdOpe.ItAutos=5,"S"," "))
_AltDescricao = ( crSigCdOpe.ItAutos = 3 )
_BaixaSubN    = ( crSigCdOpe.BaixaSns = 1 )
_Posicao      = TprMvCab.Emps + TprMvCab.Dopes + Str( TprMvCab.Numes, 6 )
_BxParc       = (crSigCdOpe.BxParcials = 1)
_BxAutos      = (crSigCdOpe.BxAutos = 1)
_PuxaOrigem   = crSigCdOpe.ConOSubs
_PuxaDestino  = crSigCdOpe.ConDSubs
_ChecaEntrega = crSigCdOpe.ChDtentrs = 1
_ChecaPends   = crSigCdOpe.ExPends
lnCasas	      = iif(IsEmpty(crSigCdOpe.Casas),2,crSigCdOpe.Casas)
_MoePadrao    = crSigCdOpe.Cmoes
_TabPadrao    = ( crSigCdOpe.TabDescs = 5 )
_SubnObrig    = ( crSigCdOpe.SubnObrigs )
_ChecaAgenda  = ( CrSigCdOpe.chkAgends )
_CarObs       = Not( CrSigCdOpe.ConsClis = 2 )
_AnProducao   = ( CrSigCdOpe.AnProdzs = 1 )
_CarOpCr      = CrSigCdOpe.Opcrs
_CarEmpDs     = ( CrSigOpCdc.CarESubns = 1 )
_CarEmpDsSubn = ( CrSigOpCdd.EmpDesSubn = 1 )
_TrocaPrd     = ( crSigCdOpe.ItAutos )
_ChkCtEntrega = InList(crSigCdOpe.DtEntrs, 3, 4, 5, 6)
_MaxSubNivel  = Iif(CrSigOpCdc.MaxSubns <= 0, 9999, CrSigOpCdc.MaxSubns )
_ChkEntregaSN = ( crSigOpCdc.ChDteSns <> 1 )
_ChkEmpDestino = ( CrSigOpCdc.chkEmpDs = 1 )
_CfoAtual      = crSigCdOpe.Cfos
_CompSubn     = ( crSigCdOpe.CempSubns = 1 )
_MercSep      = ( CrSigOpCdc.MercSeps )
_ReplicaDes   = ( crSigCdOpe.cRepls = 1 )

_CarDesObs    = (Type([CrSigOpCdd.DescOb2s])=[N] And CrSigOpCdd.DescOb2s= 1)

Select [ ] as Agrupar,Sum(1) as Qt From TmpOperacao ;
	Where Codigos > 0 ;
Into Cursor CheckMax group by 1

Select TmpPends
_RecnoPend = Recno()
_Conta = 0
_QtdSubn = 0
Scan
	If Flag Or Recno() = _RecnoPEnd
		_Conta = _Conta + 1
	Endif
Endscan
Go _RecnoPend

_Conta = CheckMax.Qt + 1

If ( _NumeroAnt Or _DigDocu Or _PedClis ) And (_Conta > 1)
	Messagebox( 'Este tipo de operação só aceita 1 subnível...', 48, '' )
	Return .f.
EndIf

If _Conta > _MaxSubNivel
	If !_Automatico
		MessageBox("Número Máximo de Pendências Excedido!!!",0+16, "ATENÇÃO" )
	Endif
	Return (.f.)
Endif

If oPendSnope.tStatus = 1 AND (_Conta > 1)
	Messagebox( 'Movimentação com Informações de Status só aceita 1 subnível...', 48, '' )
	Return .f.
EndIf

_QtdSubn = _Conta

Select crSigCdPam
Go Top

llSubn = .t.
llDtAlt = Datetime()
llDtBx  = TprMvCab.Datas

*!*	lcUpDate = [Update SigMvCab Set ChkBxParcs = ChkBxParcs Where EmpDopNums = ']+_Nivel+[']
*!*	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'') < 1)
*!*		=MessageBox('Registro em Uso por Outro Usuario', 16, 'Falha na Conexão ')
*!*		Return .f.
*!*	Endif

lcSql = [Select ChkSubn, Contads, Contaos, Grupoos, Grupods, prazoents, dopes, notas, ecfs, obses, CodObs, numes, EmpDs, ] + ;
		[mascnum, grvends, Vends, Grresps, Resps, lprecos, tabds, tpfats, espes, qtdes, pliqs, pbrus, MoeIts, ValVarPs, ] + ;
		[valvars, vars, datas, valos, DtEmis, CodTrans, LocalEnts, EmpdopNums, ContaEs, ValnDevs, ValInis, Emps, Nemps, ] +;
		[Localizas, DtAgends, Utilizados, ChkPagos, MotDscs, ValoBxs, ValTrans, fpubls, CidChaves as CidChaveAnts, ] +;
		[Descs, Acres, Jobs, PTax1s, PTax2s, PTax3s, ObsCabMovs, Jobs, Lcancelas, pStatus, pedidoweb, IDEntrega, CDRastreio, ] + ;
		[nlivdg, grupoccs, contaccs, ChaveNota, IdTransp, NumeroNota, EmpRet ] + ;
		[From SigMvCab ] + ;
		[Where EmpDopNums = '] + _Nivel + [']

If Thisform.ParentForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrTprMvCab') < 1
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTprMvCab)')
	Return .F.
EndIf

CidChav = CrTprMvCab.CidChaveAnts
*!*	lcUpdate = [UpDate SigMvCab with(ROWLOCK) Set Dgopes = Dgopes Where CidChaves = ']+CidChav+[']
lcUpdate = [UpDate SigMvCab Set EmpDopNums = EmpDopNums Where CidChaves = ']+CidChav+[']
If (Thisform.ParentForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcUpdate, '') < 1)
	Messagebox('Movimentação sendo utilizada por outro usuário!',0+48,'')
	Return .f.
Endif

*** Soma Os valores para checagem do Limite da Operação.
lnValLbOpe = 0
If CrTmpOpe2.ChkLimOps = 1
	lcMoeP = crSigCdPam.MoedaPs
	lnCotP = ThisForm.parentForm.Cotacao(lcMoeP, TprMvCab.Datas)

	Select [ ] as Agrupar,Sum(VlLibOpes) as TtOpe From TmpOperacao Where codigos > 0 ;
	Into Cursor Selecao group by 1
	
	_ttOpe = Selecao.TtOpe

	CotPen = Iif(Empty(lcMoePend),1,ThisForm.ParentForm.Cotacao(lcMoePend, TprMvCab.Datas ) )
	_TtPen = CrTprMvCab.Valos * CotPen
	_TtGer = ( (_ttOpe + _TtPen ) / lnCotP )
	
	If Not ThisForm.ParentForm.LiberaPagto(_TtGer, lcMoeP, TprMvCab.datas)
		Return .f.
	Endif

	lnValLbOpe = _ttPen

Endif

lcQuery = [Select Series,VlFretes,FrtIpis,FrtIcms,VlSeguros,SegIpis,SegIcms,DespAces,DesIpis,DesIcms,Cfis,nFis,EspecieNfs,emis,nfechv,totnotas,totprods ] + ;
	[From SigMvNfi Where EmpDopNums = ']+ _Nivel +[']
If Thisform.ParentForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpNfis') < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpNfis)')
	Select TmpOperacao
	Return .F.
EndIf

If (crSigOpCdd.nCarFrts = 1)
	Replace VlFretes With 0,;
			FrtIpis  With 0,;
			FrtIcms  With 0 In CrTmpNfis
EndIf
If (crSigOpCdd.nCardesps = 1)
	Replace DespAces With 0,;
			DesIpis  With 0,;
			DesIcms  With 0 In CrTmpNfis
EndIf
If (crSigOpCdd.nCardscs = 1)
	Replace VlSeguros With 0,;
			SegIpis   With 0,;
			SegIcms   With 0 In CrTmpNfis
EndIf

If CrSigCdOpe.CupFis = 1 And ( CrTmpNfis.VlFretes + CrTmpNfis.VlSeguros + CrTmpNfis.DespAces ) <> 0
	MessageBox('Não é Permitido Emitir Cupom Fiscal '+Chr(13)+'com Valor de Frete / Seguro / Despesas Acessorias',0+16,'')
	Select TmpOperacao
	Return .F.
Endif

lcSql = [Select * From SigMvEtv Where EmpDopNums = '] + _Nivel + [']
If Thisform.ParentForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrTprMvCabV') < 1
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTprMvCabV)')
	Return .F.
EndIf



lcSql = [Select * From SigCdTif Where Codigos = ']+TmpPends.TpFats+[']
If Thisform.ParentForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrSigCdTif') < 1
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrSigCdTif)')
	Return .F.
EndIf

_Retorna = .t.

Do Case
	Case InList(crSigCdOpe.ApTpFats,1,4) And crSigCdTif.PQtdes = 100 And !Inlist( crSigCdOpe.NFiscals, 1, 2 ) And crSigCdOpe.NDebitos # 1
		If !_Automatico
			MessageBox( "Não é possível fazer a venda! Emita a NF...", 48, "ATENÇÃO" )
		Endif
		_Retorna = .f.

	Case InList(crSigCdOpe.ApTpFats,1,4) And crSigCdTif.PValors = 100 And crSigCdOpe.NDebitos = 1
		If !_Automatico
			MessageBox( "Não é possível emitir uma Nota de Débito...", 48, "ATENÇÃO" )
		Endif
		_Retorna = .f.
		
	Case CrTprMvCab.chksubn And InList(crSigCdOpe.Subns,1,4,5)
		If !_Automatico
			Messagebox('Movimentação já utilizada em outra operação !!!',0+48,'')
		Endif
		_Retorna = .f.

	Case crSigCdOpe.fechmals <> 1 and !IsEmpty( TprMvCab.Vends ) and CrTprMvCab.Vends # TprMvCab.Vends &&And (crSigCdOpe.CupFis <> 1)
		If !_Automatico
			Messagebox('Movimentação de Outro ' + Alltrim(ThisForm.CfgTitVendedor) + '!!!',0+48,'')
		Endif
		_Retorna = .f.
		
	Case crSigCdOpe.fechmals <> 1 and !IsEmpty( TprMvCab.Contads ) and CrTprMvCab.Contads # TprMvCab.Contads And !_Conserto And !_NumeroAnt And OpeAtu = 2 And (crSigCdOpe.cupfis <> 1)
		If !_Automatico
			Messagebox('Movimentação de outra conta !!!',0+48,'')
		Endif
		_Retorna = .f.
		
	Case crSigCdOpe.fechmals <> 1 and !IsEmpty( TprMvCab.Contaos ) and CrTprMvCab.Contaos # TprMvCab.Contaos And !_Conserto And !_NumeroAnt And OpeAtu = 1
		If !_Automatico
			Messagebox('Movimentação de outra conta !!!',0+48,'')
		Endif
		_Retorna = .f.

	*** mena - 08/08/2014 - não pode setar nota emitida como cancelada ***
	Case CrTmpOpe2.chklmpss = 1 and TmpPrNfe.stats = 'EMI'
		MessageBox( "Esta operação é uma NFe emitida. Cancelar primeiro na SEFAZ!", 0+48,"" )
		_Retorna = .f.

	Case crSigCdOpe.fechmals = 1 and _Conta > 1
		Select TmpPends
		nreg = Recno()
		Scan
			If TmpPends.flag and nreg <> Recno()
				cedpsub = TmpPends.Emps + TmpPends.Dopes + Str( TmpPends.Numes, 6 )
				ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Cursorquery('SigMvCab','CrEest','EmpDopNums',cedpsub, [contaos, contads, vends] )
				If CrEest.Contads # CrTprMvCab.Contads And !_Conserto And !_NumeroAnt And OpeAtu = 2 And (crSigCdOpe.cupfis <> 1)
					If !_Automatico
						Messagebox('Movimentação de outra conta !!!',0+48,'')
					Endif
					_Retorna = .f.				
				EndIf
				If CrEest.Contaos # CrTprMvCab.Contaos And !_Conserto And !_NumeroAnt And OpeAtu = 1
					If !_Automatico
						Messagebox('Movimentação de outra conta !!!',0+48,'')
					Endif
					_Retorna = .f.				
				EndIf
			EndIf
		EndScan
		Go nreg

	Case (_Conta > 1) And (crTprMvCab.CodTrans <> TprMvCab.CodTrans) And Not _Conserto And Not _NumeroAnt
		If Not _Automatico
			=MessageBox('Movimentação de Outra Transportadora!!!', 0+48, '')
		EndIf
		_Retorna = .f.

	Case (_Conta > 1) And (crTprMvCab.LocalEnts <> TprMvCab.LocalEnts And Not Empty(TprMvCab.LocalEnts)) And ;
			Not _Conserto And Not _NumeroAnt And _ChkCtEntrega
		If Not _Automatico
			=MessageBox('Movimentação Com Outro Local de Entrega!!!', 0+48, '')
		EndIf
		_Retorna = .f.

	Case (_Conta > 1) And (crTprMvCab.ContaEs <> TprMvCab.ContaEs And Not Empty(TprMvCab.ContaEs)) And ;
			Not _NumeroAnt And _ChkCtEntrega
		If Not _Automatico
			=MessageBox('Movimentação Com Outra Conta de Entrega!!!', 0+48, '')
		EndIf
		_Retorna = .f.

	Case Not Empty(CrTprMvCab.EmpDs) And CrTprMvCab.Empds <> TprMvCab.Emps And _ChkEmpDestino
		If Not _Automatico
			MessageBox('Movimentação de Outra Empresa Destino!!!',0+48,'')
		Endif
		_Retorna = .f.

	Case IsEmpty(crTprMvCab.DtAgends) And _checaAgenda = 1
		If Not _Automatico
			MessageBox('Movimentação sem Agendamento!!!',0+48,'')
		Endif
		_Retorna = .f.

	Case Not IsEmpty(crTprMvCab.DtAgends) And _checaAgenda = 3
		If Not _Automatico
			MessageBox('Movimentação Com Agendamento!!!',0+48,'')
		Endif
		_Retorna = .f.

	Case Not crTprMvCab.ChkSubN And crSigCdOpe.SubNs=3 And CrSigOpCdc.NCarBxs = 1
		If Not _Automatico
			MessageBox('Movimentação não Baixada!!!',0+48,'')
		Endif
		_Retorna = .f.

	* Richard em 01/07/2015 - Carregar Somente Subníveis Cancelados
	Case Not crTprMvCab.LCancelas And (Int(Val(Substr(crSigOpCdd.ConfigGers, 216, 1))) = 1)
		If Not _Automatico
			MessageBox([A Movimentação Não Está Cancelada!!!], 0+48, [Atenção!!!])
		EndIf
		_Retorna = .f.

	Case CrTprMvCab.Empds # TprMvCab.Empds And _CompSubn
		If Not _Automatico
			MessageBox('Movimentação de Outra Empresa!!!',0+48,'')
		Endif
		_Retorna = .f.

	Case CrTprMvCab.ChkPagos And crSigCdOpe.ChecPagos = 2
		If Not _Automatico
			MessageBox('Movimentação já Paga!!!',0+48,'')
		Endif
		_Retorna = .f.

	Case Not CrTprMvCab.ChkPagos And crSigCdOpe.ChecPagos = 1
		If Not _Automatico
			MessageBox('Movimentação não Paga!!!',0+48,'')
		Endif
		_Retorna = .f.

	Case _MercSep = 1 And CrTprMvCab.Utilizados <> 1
		If Not _Automatico
			MessageBox('Movimentação Com Mercadoria Não Separada!!!',0+48,'')
		Endif
		_Retorna = .f.

	Case _MercSep = 3 And CrTprMvCab.Utilizados <> 0
		If Not _Automatico
			MessageBox('Movimentação Com Mercadoria Separada!!!',0+48,'')
		Endif
		_Retorna = .f.

	Case Inlist( _ChecaPends, 1 , 2, 3 ) And ( IsEmpty( TprMvCab.Contaos ) Or IsEmpty( TprMvCab.Contads )  )
		If Inlist( _ChecaPends, 1, 3 ) And IsEmpty( TprMvCab.Contaos )
			Store '' to _lcGrupo, _lcConta
			If InList( _PuxaOrigem, 1, 2 )
				_lcGrupo = Iif( _PuxaOrigem=1, CrTprMvCab.Grupoos, CrTprMvCab.Grupods )
				_lcConta = Iif( _PuxaOrigem=1, CrTprMvCab.Contaos, CrTprMvCab.Contads )
			Endif
			If !ThisForm.ParentForm.MostraPends( _lcGrupo, _lcConta )
				_Retorna = .f.
			Endif
		Endif
		If Inlist( _ChecaPends, 2, 3 ) And IsEmpty( TprMvCab.Contads )
