================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPEN.Cnt_Status.Commandgroup1
* Classe: commandgroup (Base: commandgroup)
* Registro: 89
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Command1.Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Command1.Click
Private pDat

pDat = Datetime()

lcBusca = TmpPends.Emps + TmpPends.Dopes + Str(TmpPends.Numes,6)

ThisForm.StatusAprRep = Substr(crSigOpCdd.StsGrvs,1,1)

If Not Empty(ThisForm.cnt_Status.txtObses.Value)
	lcObs = Alltrim(Iif(IsEmpty(TmpPends.Obss), [], Nvl(TmpPends.Obss, [])))
	If Not Empty(lcObs)
		lcObs = lcObs + Chr(13)
	EndIf
	lcObs = lcObs + [Aprovação : ] + Alltrim(ThisForm.cnt_Status.txtObses.Value) + [ (] + Alltrim(Usuar) + [)]

	lcUpDate = [Update SigMvCab Set Usuals = '] + Usuar + [', ] + ;
								   [DtAlts = ?pDat, ] + ;
								   [Obses = '] + lcObs + [', ] + ;
								   [pStatus = '] + Substr(crSigOpCdd.StsGrvs,1,1) + [' ] + ;
								   [Where EmpDopNums = ']+lcBusca+[']
	Replace Obses With lcObs In TprMvCab
Else
	lcObs = [Aprovado!]
	lcUpDate = [Update SigMvCab Set Usuals = '] + Usuar + [', ] + ;
								   [DtAlts = ?pDat, ] + ;
								   [Obses = '] + lcObs + [', ] + ;
								   [pStatus = '] + Substr(crSigOpCdd.StsGrvs,1,1) + [' ] + ;
								   [Where EmpDopNums = ']+lcBusca+[']
EndIf

If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Registro em Uso por Outro Usuario', 16, 'Falha na Conexão 1')
	Replace Flag With .F. in TmpPends
	=ThisForm.Desmarca()
	Return .f.
EndIf

lcTam = ThisForm.cnt_Status.getCodTams.Value
If Not Empty(lcTam)
	lcUpDate = [Update SigMvIts Set CodTams = '] + lcTam + [' Where EmpDopNums = ']+lcBusca+[']
	Update crTpmMvIts Set CodTams = lcTam Where EmpDopNums = lcBusca

	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Registro em Uso por Outro Usuario', 16, 'Falha na Conexão 1')
		Replace Flag With .F. in TmpPends
		=ThisForm.Desmarca()
		Return .f.
	EndIf
EndIf

Insert Into crStsMvPec (EmpDopNums, pStatus, Codigos) Values (TprMvCab.Emps + TprMvCab.Dopes + Str(TprMvCab.Numes,6), TmpPends.pStatus, TmpOperacao.Codigos)

If Seek(lcBusca,'TmpPendR','EmpDopNums')
	Replace pStatus With Substr(crSigOpCdd.StsGrvs,1,1) in TmpPendR &&[ ]
	Replace CodTams With Substr(crSigOpCdd.StsGrvs,1,1) in TmpPendR &&[ ]
EndIf
Replace pStatus With Substr(crSigOpCdd.StsGrvs,1,1) in TmpPends && [ ]
ThisForm.SelecStatus = .t.

ThisForm.Cnt_Status.LostFocus()

If CrSigOpCdd.GrvAutos = 1
	ThisForm.cntBotoes.btnSair.Click()
EndIf
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Command2.Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Command2.Click
Private pDat
pDat = Datetime()

lcBusca = TmpPends.Emps + TmpPends.Dopes + Str(TmpPends.Numes,6)

If Empty(ThisForm.cnt_Status.txtObses.Value)
	=MessageBox([Necessário Informar o Motivo da Reprovação!], 0+48, [Motivo Não Preenchido])
	Return 0
EndIf

lcObs = Alltrim(Iif(IsEmpty(TmpPends.Obss), [], Nvl(TmpPends.Obss, [])))
If Not Empty(lcObs)
	lcObs = lcObs + Chr(13)
EndIf
lcObs = lcObs + [Reprovação : ] + ThisForm.cnt_Status.txtObses.Value + [ (] + Alltrim(Usuar) + [)]

*Replace Flag With .F. in TmpPends
Replace Obses With lcObs In TprMvCab

ThisForm.StatusAprRep = Substr(crSigOpCdd.StsGrvs,2,1)

lcUpDate = [Update SigMvCab Set Usuals = '] + Usuar + [', ] + ;
							   [DtAlts = ?pDat, ] + ;
							   [Obses = '] + lcObs + [', ] + ;
							   [pStatus = '] + Substr(crSigOpCdd.StsGrvs,2,1) + [' ] + ;
							   [Where EmpDopNums = ']+lcBusca+[']

If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Registro em Uso por Outro Usuario', 16, 'Falha na Conexão ')
	=ThisForm.Desmarca()
	Return .f.
EndIf
Replace pStatus With Substr(crSigOpCdd.StsGrvs,2,1) in TmpPends &&[R]

lcTam = ThisForm.cnt_Status.getCodTams.Value
If Not Empty(lcTam)
	lcUpDate = [Update SigMvIts Set CodTams = '] + lcTam + [' Where EmpDopNums = ']+lcBusca+[']
	Update crTpmMvIts Set CodTams = lcTam Where EmpDopNums = lcBusca

	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Registro em Uso por Outro Usuario', 16, 'Falha na Conexão 1')
		Replace Flag With .F. in TmpPends
		=ThisForm.Desmarca()
		Return .f.
	EndIf
EndIf

If Not Seek(lcBusca,'TmpPendR','EmpDopNums')
	Insert Into TmpPendR (EmpDopNums,pStatus,CodTams) Values (lcBusca,Substr(crSigOpCdd.StsGrvs,2,1),lcTam) &&[R]
EndIf
Replace Obses With lcObs In TmpPendR
Replace pStatus With Substr(crSigOpCdd.StsGrvs,2,1) in TmpPendR &&[R]
Replace CodTams With lcTam in TmpPendR &&[R]
ThisForm.SelecStatus = .t.

*ThisForm.Desmarca()
ThisForm.Cnt_Status.LostFocus()

If CrSigOpCdd.GrvAutos = 1
	ThisForm.cntBotoes.btnSair.Click()
EndIf
ENDPROC

							   [Obses = '] + lcObs + [', ] + ;
							   [pStatus = '] + Substr(crSigOpCdd.StsGrvs,3,1) + [' ] + ;
							   [Where EmpDopNums = ']+lcBusca+[']

If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Registro em Uso por Outro Usuario', 16, 'Falha na Conexão ')
	=ThisForm.Desmarca()
	Return .f.
EndIf
Replace pStatus With Substr(crSigOpCdd.StsGrvs,3,1) in TmpPends &&[C]

lcTam = ThisForm.cnt_Status.getCodTams.Value
If Not Empty(lcTam)
	lcUpDate = [Update SigMvIts Set CodTams = '] + lcTam + [' Where EmpDopNums = ']+lcBusca+[']
	Update crTpmMvIts Set CodTams = lcTam Where EmpDopNums = lcBusca

	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Registro em Uso por Outro Usuario', 16, 'Falha na Conexão 1')
		Replace Flag With .F. in TmpPends
		=ThisForm.Desmarca()
		Return .f.
	EndIf
EndIf

If Not Seek(lcBusca,'TmpPendR','EmpDopNums')
	Insert Into TmpPendR (EmpDopNums,pStatus,CodTams) Values (lcBusca,Substr(crSigOpCdd.StsGrvs,3,1),lcTam) &&[C]
EndIf
Replace Obses With lcObs In TmpPendR
Replace CodTams With lcTam In TmpPendR
Replace pStatus With Substr(crSigOpCdd.StsGrvs,3,1) in TmpPendR &&[C]
ThisForm.SelecStatus = .t.

ThisForm.Desmarca()

ThisForm.Cnt_Status.LostFocus()

ENDPROC

PROCEDURE Valid
Local loLista

If Not Empty(This.Value)
	loLista = CreateObject([fwbuscaext], ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.pnIdConn, [SigCdTam], ;
						   [crListaRemota], [Cods], This.Value, [Seleção], .t.)

		loLista.mAddColuna([Descs], [], [Descrição])
		loLista.Show()
	EndIf
	
	This.Value = Iif(Lastkey()=27, [], crListaRemota.Cods)
	Use In crListaRemota
EndIf
This.Refresh
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPEN.CntBars.btnBarDisp
* Classe: fwbtnp (Base: commandbutton)
* Registro: 98
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
LOCAL lcEdn, lcMsg, lnOldSel, lnReg

lnOldSel = Select()
lnReg = RECNO()

lcEdn = TmpPends.Emps + TmpPends.Dopes + Str(TmpPends.Numes,6)

IF INT(VAL(SUBSTR(crSigOpCdd.ConfigGers, 237,1)))=1	&& mena 01/12/2016 - apresenta barras disponíveis
	lcSql = [Select cbars from sigopetq where empdopnums = ']+lcEdn+[' and empos = ']+TmpPends.Emps+[']

	If Thisform.ParentForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql, [crBar]) < 1
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crBar)')
		Return .F.
	EndIf
	SELECT ALLTRIM(TRANSFORM(cbars, '@R 99999999999999')) as barra from crBar into cursor crBar
	Go Top In crBar
ENDIF

IF INT(VAL(SUBSTR(crSigOpCdd.ConfigGers, 237,1)))=2	&& mena 01/12/2016 - apresenta referencia dos produtos do subnivel
*!*		SELECT tmpPends
*!*		GO top
*!*		lcEdn = []
*!*		SCAN for flag
*!*			lcEdn = lcEdn + [']+TmpPends.Emps + TmpPends.Dopes + Str(TmpPends.Numes,6)+[',]	
*!*		ENDSCAN
	IF !EMPTY(lcEdn)
		*!*lcEdn = [(]+SUBSTR(lcEdn,1,LEN(lcEdn)-1)+[)]
		lcSql = [Select cpros as barra from sigmvitn where empdopnums = ']+lcEdn+[' order by cpros]
		If Thisform.ParentForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql, [crBar]) < 1
			=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crBar)')
			Return .F.
		EndIf
	ENDIF
ENDIF

With ThisForm.cntBars.grdBar 
	.Enabled       =.t.
	.visible       =.t.
	.FontName      = 'Courier New'
	.FontSize      = 8
	.RecordSource  = 'CrBar'
	.Column1.ControlSource = 'CrBar.barra'
	.Column1.Width = 200
	.HeaderHeight  = 18
	.colUMN1.hEADER1.Caption=IIF(INT(VAL(SUBSTR(crSigOpCdd.ConfigGers, 237,1)))=2,'Referências','Barras Disponíveis')
	.RowHeight = 18
	.Refresh
EndWith

SELECT(lnOldSel)
GO lnReg

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPEN.getLocPed
* Classe: textbox (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 104
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
If !Empty(This.Value)
	This.Value = []
	ThisForm.Grade.Setfocus
	ThisForm.Grade.seleciona.Setfocus
Endif

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If !Empty( This.Value )
	If Not ThisForm.CarItem
		Select TmpPends
		Set Order To PedidoWeb
		If Not Seek(This.Value)
			ThisForm.Carregar(0,'',This.Value)
		EndIf
	Endif
	Select TmpPends
	Set Order to PedidoWeb
	Set Near On 
	Seek This.Value
	Set Near Off
	If TmpPends.PedidoWeb = This.Value And CrSigCdOpe.MarcaSubns = 1
		Scan While PedidoWeb = This.Value
			=ThisForm.Marca()
		EndScan
	Endif
	ThisForm.Refresh
EndIf

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPEN.GetTransp
* Classe: textbox (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 109
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local loLista
If !Empty(This.Value)
	loLista = CreateObject('fwbuscaext', ThisForm.Parentform.poDataMgr.pnIdConn, 'SigPrTrp',  'crListaRemota', 'iTras', This.Value, 'Seleção', .T.)
	If Not loLista.plAchouRegistro
		loLista.mAddColuna('iTras', '', 'Código')
		loLista.mAddColuna('Razaos', '', 'Descrição')
		loLista.mAddColuna('IdTransp', '', 'ID')
		loLista.Show()
	EndIf
	This.Value = crListaRemota.iTras
	Use In crListaRemota
Else
	This.Value = []
EndIf

*If !Empty( This.Value )
	Select TmpPends
	Zap
	Set Order To IDTransp
	ThisForm.Carregar(0,'','',This.Value)
	
	Select TmpPends
	Set Order to IDTransp
	Set Near On 
	Seek This.Value
	Set Near Off
	If TmpPends.IDTransp = This.Value And CrSigCdOpe.MarcaSubns = 1
		Scan While IDTransp = This.Value
			=ThisForm.Marca()
		EndScan
	Endif
	ThisForm.Refresh
*EndIf

ENDPROC

* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
If !Empty(This.Value)
	This.Value = []
	ThisForm.Grade.Setfocus
	ThisForm.Grade.seleciona.Setfocus
Endif

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPEN.getCpros
* Classe: textbox (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 112
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local loLista
If !Empty(This.Value)
	loLista = CreateObject('fwbuscaext', ThisForm.Parentform.poDataMgr.pnIdConn, 'SigCdPro',  'crListaRemota', 'Cpros', This.Value, 'Seleção', .T.)
	If Not loLista.plAchouRegistro
		loLista.mAddColuna('Cpros', '', 'Código')
		loLista.mAddColuna('Dpros', '', 'Descrição')
		loLista.Show()
	EndIf
	This.Value = crListaRemota.Cpros
	Use In crListaRemota
Else
	This.Value = []
EndIf

Select TmpPends
Zap
ThisForm.Carregar(0,'','','',This.Value)
	
This.Value = []

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPEN.BtnLiberar
* Classe: commandbutton (Base: commandbutton)
* Registro: 114
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
lcUsuLibs = []
Messagebox('Liberar Consulta de Todos Subniveis somente com Autorização!!',0+48,'ERRO')
Do Form SigOpSen With 'LIBSUBN','Libera Consulta de Todos Subniveis',"" To _Retorno
llOk = ( Left( _Retorno, 1 ) = "*" )
If !llOk
	MessageBox('Autorização não Confirmada!!',0+48,'')
	Return 0
EndIf
lcUsuLibs = Substr(_Retorno,2,10)

If Not Empty(lcUsuLibs)
	Select TmpPends
	Zap
	ThisForm.Refresh
	ThisForm.Carregar(0,'','','','',.T.)
EndIf 


ENDPROC
